We further review the core technical methods these systems employ: TTS-based exemplar generation and diagnostic methods for pronunciation, prosody, and fluency assessment. Key open challenges include the scarcity of annotated presentation corpora, achieving accent-fair feedback across diverse L1 backgrounds, and delivering low-latency diagnostics for real-time rehearsal.

</details>


### [29] [Causal Connections: Leveraging Multilingual Fine-Tuning for Financial QA@FinCausal 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27446)
*Akash Kumar Gautam,Serhii Hamotskyi,Christian Hänig*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了HSA_CORAL团队参加FinCausal 2026共享任务的方案，针对金融文本中的因果关系抽取任务（英西双语），比较了三种模型架构：基于编码器的BERT、多语BART编解码器架构以及Llama 3.1和GPT系列的解码器大语言模型。最终通过GPT-4.1 Mini在多语数据上微调取得了最佳成绩：英语子任务最高分4.8140，西班牙语子任务第三名4.7753。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融文本中的因果关系识别对投资决策和风险分析至关重要。FinCausal 2026任务引入了新的评估方式（LLM-as-a-judge，1-5分制），以及更复杂的因果结构，作者旨在探索多语微调、提示工程和少样本学习在该任务上的有效性，并验证跨语言迁移能力。

Method: 论文比较了三类方法：(1) 基于多语BERT的token分类方法（IO标注方案，交叉熵损失加loss mask）；(2) 基于多语BART的seq2seq生成方法（beam search解码）；(3) 基于解码器LLM（Llama 3.1 + LoRA、GPT-3.5/GPT-4.1/GPT-5.2）的生成方法，结合提示优化、基于余弦相似度的动态少样本选择（20个样本）以及监督微调（2000个样本）。微调实验包括仅英语、仅西班牙语和双语混合三种配置。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 编码器-解码器（BART）优于纯编码器（BERT）；(2) 解码器LLM配合少样本和微调整体最优；(3) GPT-4.1 Mini多语微调取得英语任务最高分（4.8140）和西班牙语第三名（4.7753），超过了零样本的GPT-5.2；(4) 少样本数量在20个时效果最佳，超过后增益饱和甚至引发幻觉；(5) 多语微调优于单语微调，证明跨语言迁移有效。错误主要出现在嵌套因果结构和多候选原因的复杂场景中。

Conclusion: 结论表明，对于专业领域（如金融因果QA）任务，经过微调的中等规模生成模型可以优于纯推理模式的大模型，多语微调和少样本学习的组合是金融因果关系抽取的可靠方法。未来工作可探索基于偏好的对齐方法（如DPO和RLHF）以处理复杂因果结构，并使用LLM评估进一步提升输出质量。

Abstract: This paper describes team HSA_CORAL's submission to the FinCausal 2026 shared task on extracting cause-effect relations from financial narratives via extractive question answering in English and Spanish. We compare three modeling families: (i) encoder-only token tagging with multilingual BERT, (ii) encoder-decoder generation with multilingual BART, and (iii) decoder-only LLMs (Llama 3.1 and GPT variants) using prompt refinement, few-shot demonstrations, and supervised fine-tuning. Across settings, prompting and few-shot examples yield competitive performance, while supervised fine-tuning provides the largest gains. Our best system, GPT-4.1 Mini fine-tuned on combined English and Spanish training data, achieves a tied highest score on the English subtask (score 4.8140) and ranks third on Spanish (score 4.7753) under the shared task's LLM-as-a-judge metric. Overall, the results highlight the value of task-specific adaptation and multilingual fine-tuning for cross-lingual transfer in financial causality QA.

</details>


### [30] [Developmental approach reveals the statistical learning of Neural Language Models: Transformers generalize from the most abstract statistical patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27460)
*Wang Bojun,Holly Jenkins,Elizabeth Wonnacott*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究采用发展心理学方法，通过训练生成式Transformer模型在具有三层嵌套依赖关系（全局、中间、局部）的合成语法上，发现神经语言模型（NLM）采用"过度泛化"学习路径：先习得最抽象的全局统计规律，再逐步约束学习相对局部的统计依赖。模型在训练早期就出现系统性的过度泛化现象，且这一模式在不同词序排列的语法中具有稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多借鉴人类语言认知理论（生成语言学或功能语言学）来解释NLM的内部表征，但这些理论框架基于人类特有的先天偏见（如普遍语法、语义-句法分离），不适用于纯统计学习的NLM。本研究旨在通过发展路径视角，直接考察NLM的统计学习机制，探究其是采用"过度泛化路径"（先全局后局部）还是"保守路径"（先局部后全局），为理解NLM的基本表征形式提供实证基础。

Method: 1. 设计合成语法：构建三层嵌套继承层次结构（全局类别M/N/P/Q，中间类别如N₁/N₂/P₁/P₂/Q₁/Q₂，局部类别如P₁₁/P₁₂/Q₁₁/Q₁₂等），加入flanker类别U消除位置依赖。2. 训练小型生成式Transformer（1层、1头、4维嵌入）：单个模型训练150,000次迭代，每1,000步保存检查点；另训练100个模型用于概率质量分析。3. 表征分析：提取P和Q令牌的静态嵌入，用4-16-2-16-4自编码器降维至2D，制作运动图表可视化学习路径。4. 行为分析：计算测试集上全局/中间/局部目标类别的概率质量（probability mass），追踪100个模型在不同训练阶段的平均表现。5. 鲁棒性检验：对MNPQ四种排列组合分别训练10个模型，验证梯度模式与线性顺序无关。

Result: 1. 学习路径可视化显示：约24,000步时全局类别已习得但无子类结构；约69,000步中间类别出现；约134,000步局部类别完全习得。2. 100个模型的概率质量分析证实全局类别最早达到概率质量接近1，中间次之，局部最后，呈梯度习得模式。3. 早期阶段的过度泛化具有结构性而非无序：模型在15,000步时将所有Q类别令牌排在概率前列，但Q₁₁/Q₁₂/Q₂₁/Q₂₂子类混杂，从未出现N或P令牌。4. 6种不同词序排列的合成语法均呈现相同的全局到局部梯度模式，证明该模式与线性顺序无关。

Conclusion: NLM采用"过度泛化"学习路径：先习得最抽象的全局统计模式，再逐步约束为局部模式。早期过度泛化具有系统性结构（限制在更高阶类别内部），而非随机。NLM认知不能简单用人类语言学理论解释，需要独立的统计学习理论框架。研究支持将NLM视为依赖关系（dependency relations）的复合单元统计表征，而非先组合低层单元再形成高层抽象。研究局限包括：合成语法规模小、Transformer架构极简（1层1头4维）、未涉及真实LLM的tokenization复杂性（如字节对编码）。未来需综合多项统计学习发现构建全面的NLM认知理论。

Abstract: In this study, we use a developmental approach to investigate the statistical learning and mental representation of neural language models (NLM). A series of Generative Transformer models are trained on a synthetic grammar. The model states are saved at multiple stages in the course of training. Through analyzing how the internal representations of these models change in the developmental path, we found that NLMs acquire the most abstract global statistical knowledge at the beginning of learning and later acquire the relatively local statistical dependencies. This learning path contains many over-generalizations from the very beginning and these over-generalizations are gradually constrained in the later stage of learning. Based on this observation, we propose a new framework to explain the statistical learning and language cognition of NLMs.

</details>


### [31] [Ko-WideSearch: A Korean Breadth-Search Benchmark for Exhaustive Set Enumeration by Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27595)
*Minbyul Jeong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Ko-WideSearch 是一个韩语广度搜索基准测试，包含 228 个表格、190 个父实体、16 个类别，跨越三个难度等级（Easy/Medium/Hard），通过自动化合成-验证流水线构建。该基准评估了 20 个网页智能体在穷尽集合枚举任务上的表现，核心发现是：智能体能够恢复集合成员身份（如 GPT-5.5 的 Item-F1 达 92.8），但无法完整填充每行属性（Row-F1 仅 53.7），且这一差距在难度提升、更多搜索或更多花费下均无法弥合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页智能体基准测试主要衡量"深度"（通过多跳推理链找到单一答案），而忽视了"广度"（穷尽枚举封闭集合并填充每个项目的属性）。存在两大空白：（1）广度搜索评估在英语以外的语言（尤其是韩语）中几乎不存在，而韩语网页浏览能力具有独特的语言和文化依赖性；（2）广度基准的构建成本极高——手工验证完整集合和每个单元格的正确性比检查单一答案要昂贵得多，原始 WideSearch 即手工构建了 200 个表格，标注成本高昂。

Method: 采用自动化的合成-验证流水线构建韩语广度搜索基准：（1）任务定义——每个实例通过谓词命名一个封闭有限集合，要求填充 k 维主键 + m-k 个属性列；（2）构建智能体通过 search/open/find 工具穷尽搜索生成金标表；（3）三道独立验证关卡——非可记忆性（封闭书本模型不能从记忆中复现）、完整性（独立智能体重新枚举须与金标集合 F1≥0.7 一致）、跨源属性验证（独立查找属性，丢弃一致性低于 0.6 的列）；（4）通过两个结构旋钮控制难度——表宽度和 2-D 复合键，使 2-D 成员比例从 0% 升至 100%；（5）采用在金标构建和评分之间共享的类型感知归一化比较器，避免因格式差异（如千位分隔符、日期粒度）误判稳定列；（6）通过网页溯源的源类型标签（exhaustive-only vs cross-source）作为正交难度轴；（7）评估 20 个网页智能体（专有前沿、开源权重、韩语专用三类），每题 30 次智能体迭代预算，pass@1。

Result: 主要结果：最佳模型 GPT-5.5 达到 Item-F1 92.8、Column-F1 74.3、Row-F1 53.7、Table Success 19.3%；所有模型均出现"恢复集合但丢失行"的级联失败模式（Item-F1 普遍 70-94，Row-F1 仅 15-54）。核心发现：（1）困难度梯度陡峭——从 Easy 到 Hard，所有模型的 Row-F1 持续下降，GPT-5.5 从 58.9 降至 48.1；（2）单元格类型分析显示开放性自由文本单元格失败率最高（49），而日期（58）和名称（56）格式约束单元格最易成功，表明难点在于找到正确值而非格式化；（3）更多搜索不带来完整性——Qwen3.6 平均 66 次工具调用却得分最低（Row-F1 16.2），而 GPT-5.5 仅用 33 次即领先；（4）更多花费同样不缩小差距——DeepSeek-V4-Pro 以 GPT-5.5 四分之一的成本（$0.23 vs $0.87）达到 45.0 的 Row-F1；（5）韩语专用模型（A.X-4.0、Solar-Open-2-preview、K-EXAONE-236B）未能缩小与前沿的差距，位于开源权重底层（Row-F1 17.5-24.4）；（6）开源 DeepSeek-V4-Pro（Row-F1 45.0）保持竞争力，在专有系统中处于中游。语义判断增益随模型强度上升（强模型 +4.9，弱模型 +0.8），表明严格匹配低估了强弱系统间的差距。失败分类显示：能力强的系统瓶颈在单元格填充（56-66%），小模型在成员阶段失败（46-52%），解析失败罕见（Claude-Haiku-4.5 为 24%）。

Conclusion: Ko-WideSearch 确立了广度搜索作为网页智能体评估的独立轴心，证明了自动化可验证流水线是大规模构建可信广度基准的可行路径。核心启示是：成员身份的恢复与每行属性的完整填充是两种不同的能力，前者在现代智能体中已基本解决，后者仍是开放难题。流水线与评分器以 MIT 协议开源，评估数据按需分发（采用类似 GAIA 和 BrowseComp 的保留式发布策略），以防止智能体自身搜索泄露金标答案。局限性包括：Hard 等级过度偏向体育赛季（约 67%）、每表仅锚定一个主源 URL、网页证据随时间变化需要周期性重新验证。该基准被定位为流水线验证（带母语者抽查），而非外部金标准注释。

Abstract: Web-agent benchmarks overwhelmingly measure depth -- pinning one obscure answer behind a chain of constraints -- while breadth, exhaustively enumerating a closed set and filling each item's attributes, is barely evaluated, especially outside English. Breadth is also hard to build: certifying that a gold set is complete and every cell correct is far costlier than checking a single answer. I introduce \textsc{Ko-WideSearch}, a Korean breadth-search benchmark built by an automated synthesize-and-verify pipeline. Each task names a set-parent entity -- a TV season, a dynasty, a league, an administrative region, an election -- and asks for its full membership plus a per-item attribute table, graded by Item-, Column-, and Row-F1. It spans 228 tables over 190 entities and sixteen categories across three difficulty tiers, set by two structural knobs I dial independently -- table width and a 2-D composite key -- so cross-product membership climbs from 0\% to 100\% across the tiers. A single normalization-aware comparator is shared between gold construction and grading, so stable date and count columns are not over-dropped on formatting alone. Across twenty web agents, the failure is consistent: agents recover the set but not the rows (e.g.\ Item-F1 92.8 against Row-F1 53.7), accuracy falls steadily as the knobs harden, and neither more search nor more spend closes the gap. Broken down by cell, the hard part is finding the right value, not formatting it: open-ended free-text cells fail most, while cells with a standard answer such as a date or a name usually come out right.

</details>


### [32] [Yuvion LLM: An Adversarially-Aware Large Language Model for Content And AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27632)
*Ting Ma,Xiufeng Huang,Benlei Cui,Xiaowen Xu,Shikai Qiu,Ruijie Jian,Hongxing Li,Guanghui Wang,Longtao Huang,Haiwen Hong,Haolei Xu,Wenjing Jiang,Ziwen Xu,Zhaoyu Fan,Shaoxuan He,Chuxi Xiao,Yujian Li,Xinyue Chen,Chunyang Chai,Wenxuan Liu,Ziheng Wang,Dongjie Zhang,Yangfan Zhou,Libin Dong,Yupeng Cao,Xiaoqian Xia,Jing Wang,Zhe Jiang,Zhenan Ye,Guang Yang,Bin Liu,Wei Peng,Ziqiang Zhu,Meihui Lian,Kaiwen Lv Kacuila,Haidong Ding,Bingyu Zhu,Yan Wang,Hai Zhao,Xuan Jin,Wei Zhao,Pengfei Sun,Wei Wang,Huiming Zhang,Bin Li,Hui Xue*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Yuvion LLM 是阿里巴巴安全AGI实验室提出的面向对抗性内容安全的专用大语言模型，主张安全本质是对抗性的，并提出三阶段渐进式训练范式（知识增强的持续预训练→策略对齐的多任务安全后训练→安全感知的智能体强化学习），同时发布YLRE四层级93个基准的综合评估框架；Yuvion-8B和Yuvion-32B在公共安全、对抗鲁棒性和工业部署评估中均超越GPT-5.4和Qwen3-Max等更大规模基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用大模型的安全训练和评估主要针对自然分布输入，忽视了真实部署中违规内容生产者通过委婉化、词形替换、跨语种混合、提示注入等策略性规避手段攻击模型的问题；同时现有安全Guard模型局限于单轮安全判断，缺乏工具使用、政策检索和多步推理等智能体能力，无法满足实际内容安全场景的部署需求。论文主张对抗鲁棒性和智能体安全能力应作为模型开发的一类目标（first-class objectives）而非后处理补丁。

Method: 1) 数据系统：构建五大类数据（通用、安全领域、对抗性、智能体、合成/专家构建），平衡泛化能力与安全专精。2) 三阶段训练范式：Stage 1采用知识增强的持续预训练，将大规模安全知识库转化为多粒度（结构化三元组、句子级）训练样本；Stage 2进行策略对齐的多任务安全后训练，包含风险感知监督微调（SFT）和基于GRPO的策略优化强化学习；Stage 3实施安全感知智能体强化学习，包含工具集成推理（格式奖励+正确性奖励）和搜索增强推理（执行奖励+结果奖励）。3) YLRE四层级评估框架：Level 1公开通用基准（30+）、Level 2公开内容安全基准（8个+Guard基准28子集）、Level 3自建对抗鲁棒性基准（静态+动态，5大风险类别）、Level 4内部能力与业务基准（15+）。4) 闭式迭代框架：知识注入、对抗博弈、智能体能力强化、部署驱动反馈四大机制。

Result: 在开放内容安全基准上Yuvion-32B取得78.2% Macro F1（vs GPT-5.4 72.2%、Qwen3-Max 73.9%）；自建对抗基准静态集Avg*达94.2%，动态集组合得分20.6%（越低越好，优于GPT-5.4的22.3%）；内部业务基准Yuvion-32B达86.34，Yuvion-32B(Agent)进一步提升至87.34；智能体基准API-Bank 90.45%、BFCL 66.16%、Seal-0 40.54%，平均65.72%与Qwen3-Max(66.09%)相当；Guard基准F1从Qwen3-8B的17.6%提升至Yuvion-8B的75.1%，FPR仅0.29%；专用Guard变体Yuvion-Guard-8B在51子集上以85.6%胜率领先所有专用Guard模型；值得注意的是Yuvion-8B在多数任务上超过Qwen3-Max、GPT-5.4等大模型，证明针对性安全训练比模型规模更关键；Yuvion-32B在33个通用基准上平均0.7988（vs Qwen3-32B 0.8099），表明安全专精未显著损害通用能力。

Conclusion: Yuvion LLM证明内容安全应被视为具有独立训练范式、评估方法和部署约束的专业基础模型领域，公共基准上的安全性能系统性高估了真实部署的鲁棒性；通过将对抗鲁棒性和智能体能力作为一类目标内置到训练中，配合渐进式能力塑造和三阶段课程（知识→策略对齐→智能体），可以在保持通用能力的同时实现SOTA安全性能，并满足真实工业部署的多步推理、工具使用和策略对齐需求；现有Guard模型在内部业务基准上几乎得零分，揭示了通用安全分类与细粒度领域能力之间的根本差距，闭式迭代框架（知识注入+对抗博弈+智能体强化+部署反馈）保障模型在持续演化的对抗博弈中保持领先。

Abstract: As large language models are increasingly deployed in real-world systems, safety failures can still lead to harmful outputs and dangerous misuse. We argue that the essence of safety is adversarial: many failures arise not from natural inputs alone, but from strategic attempts to evade model policies and safeguards. However, existing general-purpose model development largely overlook this adversarial nature, and often remain insufficient for realistic safety scenarios involving planning, tool use, and multi-step reasoning, causing measured safety performance to overestimate real deployment robustness. To address this gap, we present Yuvion LLM, a large language model built for adversarially robust content safety and broader AI safety. Yuvion LLM treats adversarial robustness and agentic capability as first-class objectives. Its pipeline combines adversarially aware data construction, knowledge-enhanced continued pretraining, and policy-grounded multi-task safety post-training, including risk-aware supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning-based policy optimization, together with safety-aware agentic reinforcement learning for tool use and multi-step reasoning in complex safety scenarios. We further introduce the Yuvion LLM RiskEval (YLRE), a collection of 93 benchmarks across four evaluation categories, covering diverse open and internal evaluations with a focus on safety, adversarial robustness, and real-world capability requirements. Across these evaluations, Yuvion LLM demonstrates clear advantages on safety-focused benchmarks and particularly strong robustness under adversarial conditions, while maintaining solid overall capability. Notably, Yuvion-8B outperforms most state-of-the-art baselines, including substantially larger models such as GPT-5.4 and Qwen3-MAX, on several safety tasks.

</details>


### [33] [When Search Agents Should Ask: DiscoBench for Clarification-Aware Deep Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27669)
*Yiling Tao,Shihan Deng,Meiling Tao,Pengzhi Wei,Zhichao Hu,Zhihao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DiscoBench是一个用于评估搜索智能体在多步骤深度搜索中主动识别歧义并向用户请求澄清能力的基准测试，包含211个样本和463个歧义实例，涵盖11个领域和4种歧义类型。研究发现当前LLM搜索智能体在何时该提问、提问效果上存在明显缺陷，且反复搜索的效果甚至不如直接猜测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索智能体基准（如GAIA、BrowseComp、IN3等）通常假设用户查询是完整明确的，但现实中用户查询常模糊、不完整甚至有事实错误。在深度搜索场景下，这种歧义会沿多步骤推理链传播，导致搜索方向偏离。研究动机是填补这一空白，建立一个能评估智能体主动识别歧义、提出有效澄清问题能力的基准。

Method: 1）任务形式化：将多轮交互检索建模为序列检查点任务，每个检查点分为Unambi（明确）和Ambi（歧义，含Entity/Version/Criteria/Factual Inaccuracy四种类型），智能体可选Search/Ask/Answer三种动作。2）数据构建：采用两阶段半自动流程——种子多跳QA构建（基于11个领域人工选题+LLM扩展+人工验证），歧义注入（识别歧义点→用共享属性替换强约束→生成判别性线索）。3）评估框架：使用LLM用户模拟器逐步释放判别性线索，从任务效用、歧义检测、交互策略、成本效率四个维度评估。4）实验设计：测试11个代表性LLM，采用Neutral和Guided两种提示设置。

Result: 1）前沿模型表现有限：最佳模型Doubao-Seed-2.0-Pro在Neutral设置下仅43.1%准确率；移除搜索工具后准确率大幅下降（如Doubao从43.1%降至2.4%）。2）歧义检测与有效提问是不同能力：Qwen3.6-Max检测F1仅16.0%但CE-A达94.7%，说明会问但不知道何时问；MiniMax-M2.7频繁提问但CE-B较低。3）核心发现：SearchThenAsk策略平均通过率93.4%，显著优于DirectGuess（56.5%）和SearchHeavyGuess（51.9%）；SearchHeavyGuess甚至比直接猜测更差，揭示了"反复搜索不澄清"这一主要失败模式。4）工具调用更多不一定更好：过度检索无法可靠提升准确率。5）Guided提示能提升检测能力但端到端性能改善有限。6）Factual Inaccuracy最易检测，Entity和Criteria歧义最难。

Conclusion: DiscoBench证明当前LLM搜索智能体在澄清感知深度搜索方面仍有显著不足。模糊性检测与有效澄清提问是两种独立能力，需要单独优化。反复搜索而不主动澄清是主要失败模式，甚至不如直接猜测。未来搜索智能体不仅需要更强的检索和推理能力，还需要更好的模糊性感知和交互规划机制。论文主要局限是仅覆盖四种客观QA场景的歧义类型，且用户模拟器基于LLM而非真实人类。

Abstract: Search agents powered by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to solve complex information-seeking tasks, requiring multi-step retrieval and reasoning to fulfill user goals. However, existing benchmarks often assume that user queries are complete and explicit, overlooking the fact that real-world search requests are frequently vague, underspecified, or even factually incorrect. In deep search scenarios, such ambiguity can propagate along multi-step reasoning chains and lead agents toward incorrect search trajectories. To address this gap, we introduce DiscoBench, a benchmark for clarification-aware deep search, designed to evaluate whether search agents can proactively identify ambiguity, ask effective clarification questions, and recover correct reasoning paths through user interaction. DiscoBench contains 211 samples and 463 ambiguity instances across 11 real-world domains, covering four ambiguity types. We further design a user simulator for multi-turn interaction and evaluate model performance from four perspectives: task utility, ambiguity detection, interaction strategy, and cost efficiency. Experiments on representative LLMs show that ambiguity detection and effective clarification are distinct capabilities, and that repeatedly searching instead of asking for clarification often performs worse than direct guessing, highlighting a critical gap between retrieval ability and interactive problem-solving in current search agents.

</details>


### [34] [Mitigating Position Bias in Transformers via Layer-Specific Positional Embedding Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27705)
*Changze Lv,Zhenghua Wang,Yiran Ding,Yixin Wu,Tianlong Li,Zhibo Xu,Muling Wu,Tianyuan Shi,Shizheng Li,Qi Qian,Xuanjing Huang,Xiaoqing Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LPES（层特定位置嵌入缩放）方法，通过为Transformer每层分配独特的RoPE缩放因子来缓解"lost-in-the-middle"问题。利用Bézier曲线约束的遗传算法高效搜索最优缩放因子，在无需微调参数且不增加推理延迟的前提下，显著提升长上下文任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在处理长上下文输入时存在"lost-in-the-middle"现象，即模型过度关注首尾位置而忽略中间关键信息。现有解决方案（如Attention Buckets、Ms-PoE、MoICE）存在两大缺陷：(1) 需要多次前向传播或重复注意力计算，导致推理延迟增加；(2) 依赖人工设计的缩放策略，难以达到最优。同时，模型级集成开销大，模块级集成搜索空间过细，亟需在效率与灵活性之间取得平衡的方法。

Method: LPES采用层级别的多尺度RoPE组合策略，通过Bézier曲线将层深度与缩放因子进行参数化建模：利用少量离散控制点定义连续平滑的曲线，将组合优化搜索空间从M^L大幅压缩至(M×L)^C（减少约10^20倍）。具体包括：(1) 使用三次Bézier曲线（4个控制点）表示每层的缩放因子；(2) 设计Bézier曲线约束的遗传算法（包含交叉、变异、选择算子）优化控制点坐标，确保x坐标单调递增以保持曲线平滑；(3) 适应度函数融合首、中、尾三个子数据集的加权准确率（λ_B=0.2, λ_M=0.3, λ_E=0.5）。该方法完全无训练，不修改模型参数，仅需单次前向传播。

Result: 在Vicuna-7B-v1.5、StableBeluga-7B、Qwen2.5-7B三个模型上的实验表明：(1) 在键值检索任务上Vicuna平均准确率提升11.2%；(2) 推理速度比MoICE快2.42倍，比Ms-PoE快1.45倍，与基线相当；(3) 在ZeroSCROLLS和L-Eval长文本基准上均取得一致提升，且具备良好的跨任务、跨模型迁移能力；(4) MMLU和C-Eval测试证明模型通用能力未受损；(5) 在16K上下文窗口和Vicuna-13B大模型上仍保持有效；(6) 消融实验显示Bézier曲线优于线性和阶梯曲线，4个控制点为性能与收敛速度的最佳平衡点；(7) 表征相似性矩阵分析证实Bézier平滑缩放对模型内部表征结构扰动最小，且暴力搜索验证了平滑性作为有益归纳偏置。

Conclusion: LPES提供了一种高效、无训练、即插即用的位置偏差缓解方案，通过Bézier曲线参数化巧妙解决了组合优化难题，在保持单次前向传播效率的同时实现了更均衡的注意力分布。该方法避免了大参数更新导致的灾难性遗忘，特别适合已部署模型的优化，且在不同规模模型和上下文长度下均表现出良好的可扩展性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) still struggle with the ``lost-in-the-middle'' problem, where critical information located in the middle of long-context inputs is often underrepresented or lost. While existing methods attempt to address this by combining multi-scale rotary position embeddings (RoPE), they typically suffer from high latency or rely on suboptimal hand-crafted scaling strategies. To overcome these limitations, we introduce a layer-specific positional embedding scaling~(LPES) method that assigns distinct scaling factors to each layer. LPES achieves a more balanced attention distribution without fine-tuning model parameters or increasing inference delay. A specially designed genetic algorithm is employed to efficiently select the optimal scaling factors for each layer by incorporating Bézier curves to significantly reduce the search space. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LPES effectively mitigates positional attention bias and delivers consistent improvements across multiple long-context benchmarks, yielding up to an $11.2$\% accuracy gain on the key-value retrieval dataset.

</details>


### [35] [Learning Complementary Action Modeling from Automotive Maintenance Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27808)
*Jiaqi Wu,Bai Li,Jochen Hartmann,Martin Gaedke,Sander Stuijk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了"互补动作建模"（Complementary Action Modeling, CAM）任务，旨在通过仅修改动作短语来识别或生成汽车维护指令的程序性对应物，同时保留其余上下文。基于德国汽车维护手册构建了1,459对基准数据集，并通过候选匹配（对比学习）和受控生成（Seq2Seq）两种范式验证了该任务的可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在汽车维护指令中，动作短语的细微词法变化可能完全反转操作程序的方向（如"安装"变为"移除"），而实体、修饰语和上下文基本保持不变。这种现象对具身智能和语言引导系统至关重要，但现有程序性文本基准未能充分捕捉这一问题。CAM区别于标准语义相似度、释义生成和矛盾检测任务，需要专门建模这种基于细微词汇线索的程序性对应关系。

Method: （1）数据集构建：基于德国OEM汽车维护手册，通过层级化维修流程结构，利用德语互补动作词典（如install/remove、connect/disconnect）进行两阶段规则对齐，结合人工验证，最终获得1,459对高置信度一对一互补指令对，并标注规则可解（59%）与规则难解（41%）来源。（2）候选匹配实验：采用双编码器架构（mBERT、German BERT、多语言Sentence-Transformer、字符级Transformer），使用对称InfoNCE对比损失进行训练，通过10折交叉验证评估。（3）受控生成实验：使用mBART、mT5、Flan-T5等多语言Seq2Seq骨干网络，采用双向序列生成训练，结合嵌入空间对比正则化项。（4）评估协议：包括检索指标（R@1/5/10、MRR）、重叠指标（BLEU、ROUGE）以及人工评估语义互补性和组件一致性。

Result: 候选匹配方面：在保守的groups_native设置下，最强模型R@1约0.74，MRR约0.83；多语言Sentence-Transformer取得最佳R@1（0.7417），mBERT取得最佳MRR（0.8306）。规则可解子集R@1达0.9542，规则难解子集R@1仍达0.8237，表明学习模型能超越确定性规则。受控生成方面：mBART-large-50表现最优，BLEU达62.96、ROUGE-L达0.7970；100个人工评估样本中95个被判为语义互补，所有样本均保持组件一致性。跨车辆泛化案例研究显示64.5%的接受率，证明CAM可迁移到未见过的车辆平台。

Conclusion: CAM作为闭集匹配任务是可学习的，检索性能在多语言和德语专用编码器间表现强劲。互补维护指令应被建模为基于细微词汇线索的程序性关联，而非普通的句子相似度或释义替换。混合评估（检索指标+重叠指标+人工评估）对CAM至关重要，因为高重叠分数不能单独证明动作级转换的正确性。未来工作应扩展到更广泛的维护领域、开发动作感知自动诊断方法，并研究更具挑战性的跨文档、跨车辆泛化场景及失败案例分析。

Abstract: A minute lexical variation can reverse the procedural meaning of an instruction even when the rest of the sentence remains unchanged. In automotive maintenance instructions, this pattern often appears when an action phrase turns an instruction into its procedural counterpart. The entities, modifiers, and surrounding context remain largely invariant, while the action phrase determines the procedural relation. We define this task as Complementary Action Modeling (CAM). Given a maintenance instruction, the goal is to identify or generate its procedural counterpart by modifying the action phrase while preserving the remaining sentence context. This task focuses on three aspects: distinguishing complementarity from surface similarity, controlling generation at the action-phrase level, and evaluating relational correctness using retrieval, overlap-based, and human evaluation. Using a German automotive maintenance dataset, we examine these questions through candidate matching and controlled Seq2Seq generation. The results show that complementary maintenance instructions are best modeled as procedural associations grounded in subtle lexical cues. They should therefore not be treated as ordinary cases of sentence similarity or synonym-based paraphrasing.

</details>


### [36] [An Empirical Analysis of Factual Errors in Human-Written Text and its Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27959)
*Kazuma Iwamoto,Kazumasa Omura,Shotaro Ishihara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对人类撰写文本中的事实错误检测（FED）任务被忽视的问题，通过分析报纸文章修正案例提炼出人类特有错误分类法，并基于此评估现有大语言模型在合成与真实测试集上的事实错误检测能力，实验表明即便是高性能LLM（如GPT-5.4）在词级F1上仅得52%，揭示了该任务的艰巨性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）的兴起，研究重心转向了LLM生成文本中的幻觉检测，而对人类撰写文本中的事实错误检测研究相对不足。为填补这一研究空白，作者聚焦于人类撰写的、几乎无语法错误的报纸文章中的事实错误，并对其类别与检测难度进行系统分析。

Method: 1. 对报纸文章的修正案例进行分析，提炼出人类特有的事实错误分类法（taxonomy），识别出如"汉字误转换"（kanji misconversions）和"量词错误"（numeral classifier errors）等现有幻觉基准未涵盖的特征性错误类别。2. 基于该分类法构建合成测试集，并结合真实修正案例，对多种LLM的FED能力进行评测，包括词级F1分数等指标。3. 按检测难度进行细分分析。

Result: 实验显示，即便是高性能LLM（如GPT-5.4）在合成评估数据上仅取得52%的词级F1分数，表明该任务具有相当难度；并通过对不同检测难度的详细分析，揭示了当前FED研究的现状与挑战。

Conclusion: 人类撰写文本中的事实错误检测是一个被低估且具有挑战性的任务，现有大语言模型在该任务上表现欠佳，需要进一步研究以提升对人类特有错误（如汉字误用、量词错误等）的检测能力。

Abstract: Factual Error Detection (FED), which is the task of identifying factually incorrect spans in a given text, has long been recognized as an important research problem. However, with the rapid rise of large language models (LLMs), research attention has shifted toward factual errors specific to LLM-generated text (hallucinations) and their detection. As a result, the detection of factual errors in human-written text has been relatively neglected. To address this gap, we first distill a taxonomy of human-induced factual errors by analyzing corrections of newspaper articles, a representative source of text that is guaranteed to be human-written and contains few grammatical errors. Our analysis revealed that there are characteristic categories such as kanji misconversions and numeral classifier errors, which are not focused in existing hallucination benchmarks. Based on the taxonomy, we then evaluate the FED capability of vanilla LLMs on synthesized realistic test cases and real corrections. Experimental results demonstrated that even high-performance LLMs such as GPT-5.4 achieved only word-level F1 score of 52% on the synthetic evaluation data, highlighting the task difficulty. Furthermore, a detailed analysis by detection difficulty revealed the current state of FED.

</details>


### [37] [ToxiREX: A Dataset on Toxic REasoning in ConteXt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27981)
*Stefan F. Schouten,Ilia Markov,Piek Vossen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ToxiREX数据集，一个多语言、上下文相关的毒性推理数据集。它基于Reddit评论线程，使用结构化的毒性推理模式来捕捉和解释隐式、依赖上下文的毒性，覆盖六种语言（英语、阿拉伯语、土耳其语、西班牙语、德语和荷兰语），并提供了LLM训练集和人工标注的测试集及基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的毒性检测数据集通常缺乏对隐式和上下文依赖型毒性的捕捉能力，且很少同时支持多语言和对话上下文。为了解决这一差距，作者构建了一个统一融合多语言、对话上下文和隐式毒性标注的资源。

Method: 1. 数据采集：基于Reddit与重大事件相关的帖子（如2023年土耳其地震、俄乌冲突等）收集评论线程。2. 数据预处理：保留对话上下文。3. 标注模式：采用先前工作中开发的系统化毒性推理模式（toxic reasoning schema），对评论的隐含含义进行结构化表征，并支持映射到现有毒性分类体系。4. 数据集构建：12.5万条由商用LLM自动标注的训练集，约3000条由母语者人工标注的测试集。5. 评估策略：针对分层、基于模式的预测设计专门的评估方法。6. 基线实验：通过提示和微调语言模型进行测试。

Result: 1. 成功构建了ToxiREX数据集，是首个同时整合多语言、对话上下文和隐式毒性，并采用毒性推理模式进行丰富结构化标注的数据集。2. 测试集分析表明，标注者之间的"分歧"往往反映的是合理的替代解读，而非纯粹的标注噪声。3. 基线模型表现优于随机水平，但仍有很大的提升空间，表明该任务具有较高挑战性。

Conclusion: ToxiREX填补了多语言、上下文感知和隐式毒性检测研究资源的关键空白，为研究社区提供了更丰富、更具挑战性的毒性分析基准。其结构化标注和合理的标注分歧分析有助于推动对毒性语言更深层次的理解。然而，基线结果的不佳表现表明该任务仍需进一步研究，模型在理解隐式和上下文相关毒性方面存在显著不足。

Abstract: We introduce a new, contextual, multilingual dataset called ToxiREX: Toxic REasoning in ConteXt. The dataset consists of threads of Reddit comments and structured characterizations of what the comments imply, following a systematic toxic reasoning schema developed in a previous paper. Using the schema allows us to capture and explain implicit and context-dependent toxicity, while supporting mappings to existing toxicity taxonomies. The dataset includes comments in six languages (English, Arabic, Turkish, Spanish, German, and Dutch), collected from posts connected to specific major events (e.g. the 2023 Turkey earthquakes; the Russian invasion of Ukraine). We describe the context-preserving preprocessing of the threads. We create a training set of 125 thousand comments which is annotated by a commercially available LLM, and a test set of just under three thousand comments that is annotated by native speakers. We show that apparent disagreements in the test set annotations often reflect defensible alternative interpretations rather than noise. Finally, we provide baseline results by prompting and fine-tuning language models. To produce these results, we develop evaluation strategies for our hierarchical, schema-based predictions. While models perform better than random, there remains a lot of room for improvement, showing the task to be challenging. ToxiREX is the first dataset to simultaneously incorporate multiple languages, conversational context, and implicit toxicity, while using the toxic reasoning schema for rich, structured annotations. Dataset available at: this https URL

</details>


### [38] [The Signal-Coverage Matrix: Stratifying Type and Semantic Errors in Statement Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28013)
*Chengxiao Dai,Zhaokun Yan,Zhanhui Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出2×2信号-覆盖矩阵(信号-覆盖矩阵),将LLM自动形式化输出按Lean类型检查器(通过/失败)与语义等价判断(等价/不等价)交叉划分为四格(TS/TO/SO/BF),揭示标量TC%指标掩盖了类型层与语义层的不同修复机制。在DeepSeek V4-Pro × ProofNet#上,三种elab反馈方法(+34至+36 TS增益)约64%来自类型层恢复(TO→TS),而语义层净变化近乎持平但有87.5%的逐题替换。TO→TS比率在三种方法上均为23/61,层间恢复率具有跨方法不变性,可在2/186误差内预测其他方法的ΔTS,并在6个(模型,数据集)单元上以R²=0.96线性拟合ΔTC。Opus与GTED在elab反馈输出上分歧26-37pp(对比Vanilla的7pp),归因于elaborator迫使的结构性重写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM自动形式化的标量TC%指标(从约53%升至约76%)掩盖了不同方法实际修复了哪类错误,无法区分类型层修复与语义层修复,也无法揭示语义判断器选择对SF%报告值的显著影响(≥25pp)。论文旨在提供一个诊断框架来分解TC%增益的来源。

Method: (1) 2×2信号-覆盖矩阵:交叉Lean类型检查器(通过/失败)与Opus语义判断(等价/不等价),将每个输出分配到TS(真成功)、TO(仅类型失败)、SO(仅语义失败)、BF(双重失败)四格;同时构建4×4逐题转移矩阵追踪从Vanilla到各方法的状态变化。(2) 分层自动形式化(SAF):通过类型化JSON中间表示(IR)将自动形式化解耦,LLM生成IR,由确定性~30行模板翻译器输出Lean,elab反馈作用于IR而非Lean源码,作为因果归因探针。(3) 双判断器协议:Claude Opus 4.7(黄金跨判断器,≥94%汇总精度)与GTED(τ=0.5,符号代理,95.0%汇总精度)配对,引入四类分歧分类法(N/I/S/Z)。(4) 数据集:ProofNet#(n=186,类型复杂)与MiniF2F-test(n=244,类型简单);模型:DeepSeek V4-Pro、Qwen3.5-Plus、MiMo-v2.5-Pro;方法:Vanilla、Lean-Retry、Sample-Filter、SAF,均使用~4次LLM调用预算与K=3重试。

Result: (1) DeepSeek V4-Pro × ProofNet#上,三种elab反馈方法相对Vanilla增加34-36个TS(从95→129-131),TC%从59.7%升至75.3-76.3%。TS增益分解:23来自TO→TS(~64%类型层恢复),14来自SO→TS,2来自BF→TS,减去3个TS回归,净+36。(2) SO层净变化不大(16→10-11),但存在87.5%逐题替换(14/16被救援,新产生8个SO错误)。(3) 三种elab反馈方法的TO→TS比率均为23/61(37.7%,Wilson 95% CI [26.6%, 50.3%],Fisher精确检验p=1.00),基于Lean-Retry层间率可预测其他方法ΔTS至2/186误差内。(4) 在6个(模型,数据集)单元上,ΔTC对Vanilla elab失败率呈线性关系(R²=0.957),斜率0.325,截距2.88;ProofNet#回收率41.2%,MiniF2F回收率61.8%。(5) Opus与GTED在elab反馈输出上分歧+26至+37pp(SAF最大,因确定性翻译器隔离了LLM采样噪声),Vanilla仅+7pp;30-56%的GTED假阴性可追溯到elaborator强制的结构性重写(S类,包括绑定器/类型类/量词重排)。(6) K饱和分析:TC%在K=0→1跃升+21.5pp,K=2,3合计仅+3.3pp,三种方法饱和值差距≤1pp。(7) 持续SO残差归因于2个黄金形式化错误(Ireland-Rosen|exercise_1_30求和项不含求和变量、Dummit-Foote|exercise_7_2_2双条件LHS永真)。

Conclusion: TC%增益应按其来源格(TS/SO/TO/BF)而非标量单独归因。2×2信号-覆盖矩阵与4×4转移矩阵能区分类型层恢复与语义层替换,揭示三种算法不同的elab反馈方法在K≤3预算下收敛至同一族类型检查表面形式(层间率方法不变性)。单符号判断器SF%在elab反馈方法上系统性低估≥25pp,任何SF%声明需独立LLM验证。持续残差由基准黄金形式化错误而非LLM能力决定,亚1% SO测量需要黄金审计而非更大模型。SAF的确定性IR翻译器作为因果归因探针证实了elaborator驱动的结构重写是Opus/GTED分歧的机制。

Abstract: Headline type-correctness (TC\%) of LLM autoformalization has climbed from $\sim$53\% to $\sim$76\% in two years, yet this scalar conceals which errors each method resolves. We propose a signal-coverage matrix that crosses the Lean elaborator (pass/fail) with a semantic-equivalence judgment (equivalent/not), sorting every output into one of four cells: true success (TS), type-only (TO), semantic-only (SO), or both fail (BF). On ProofNet\# and MiniF2F-test with DeepSeek V4-Pro across Vanilla, Lean-Retry, Sample-Filter, and Stratified Autoformalization (SAF): (1) the +34 to +36 TS gain across the three elab-feedback methods is $\sim$64\% type-stratum recovery, with SO flat on net (87.5\% of original semantic errors rescued, 8 newly created). (2) The TO-to-TS rate is 23/61 for each method (Wilson 95\% CI [26.6\%, 50.3\%]), and this stratum-level recovery rate predicts $\Delta$TS on held-out methods to within 2/186 and renders $\Delta$TC linear in the Vanilla elab-fail rate across six (model, dataset) cells ($R^2=0.96$). (3) The two judges disagree by 26 to 37 pp on elab-feedback outputs (vs. 7 pp on Vanilla), with 30 to 56\% of symbolic-judge false negatives traceable to elaborator-forced rewrites. The persistent residual reduces to two gold-formalization errors. TC\% gains should be credited by which cell moved, not by the scalar alone.

</details>


### [39] [A Tree-of-Thoughts Inspired Hybrid Approach for Legal Case Judgement Summarization using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28044)
*Aniket Deroy,Kripabandhu Ghosh,Saptarshi Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受思维树（Tree-of-Thoughts, ToT）启发的提取-生成式（extractive-abstractive）混合摘要方法，用于法律判决摘要任务，并通过实验证明该方法相比纯提取式或纯生成式方法能生成更高质量的摘要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLMs）在法律领域的应用增多，法律判决摘要成为重要研究方向。现有研究大多集中在传统的提取式或生成式摘要方法上，而混合式的提取-生成方法在法律判决摘要领域尚未得到充分探索。作者希望通过结合两者的优势来提升摘要质量。

Method: 提出一种新颖的、受ToT启发的提取-生成式摘要框架。该方法将提取步骤作为思考过程的中间环节，先从原始判决文本中提取关键信息，再基于提取内容进行抽象生成。使用DeepSeek和LLaMA两种主流LLM进行实验，对比提取式、生成式和提取-生成式三种提示策略的效果。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的提取-生成式提示策略相较于纯提取式和纯生成式提示策略，能够生成质量更优的法律判决摘要。

Conclusion: 混合式的提取-生成方法是法律判决摘要任务的有效方案，ToT启发的提示设计能够引导LLM生成更准确、更连贯的判决摘要，为法律NLP应用提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: In recent times, Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being used for legal case judgement summarization. Most prior works have tried traditional extractive and abstractive summarization of case judgements. However, hybrid or extractive-abstractive techniques have not been explored much. In this work, we propose a novel tree-of-thoughts inspired extractive-abstractive summarization approach for legal judgement summarization. We conduct experiments using two popular LLMs, DeepSeek and LLama, and compare among extractive, abstractive and extractive-abstractive summarization. Our experiments show that the proposed extractive-abstractive prompt provides better summaries compared to other types of LLM prompts.

</details>


### [40] [Mechanism-Driven Monitors for Preemptive Detection of LLM Training Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28116)
*Ruixuan Huang,Yipei Wang,Wenyi Fang,Hantao Huang,Yifan Huang,Ansheng You,Zhenxing Zhang,Shuai Wang,Fan Wu,Yang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向大语言模型训练的"机制驱动"稳定性监控框架。针对低精度Flash Attention和MoE路由器两个关键模块,分别从其功能角色和故障最早可观测的计算位置推导出内部监控信号。核心方法是对QK积的增量进行双线性分解(Δ₁=Δ₂+Δ₃),监控一阶项Δ₂的谱熵;对MoE路由器则监控权重相似度和每token路由熵。故障注入实验显示,这些信号能在损失发散前数千步检测到不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大语言模型训练使用数千加速器、持续数周到数月,任何稳定性故障都代价高昂。现有监控主要依赖全局训练曲线和症状级指标(损失、梯度范数、权重范数),但这些是"延迟"指标——故障可能已经污染权重或优化器状态数千步后,损失或梯度范数才显示出异常。因此需要一种能在损失发散前归因到具体子系统的内部监控方法。

Method: 方法核心是"机制驱动监控"原则:对每个关键模块,问"该模块本应计算什么,故障最早会在哪里留下可归因痕迹"。

(1) 低精度Flash Attention监控:基于Qiu & Yao (2026)的累积机制,低精度反向误差产生相干低秩漂移ΔW。提出对QK积的增量进行双线性分解Δ₁=Δ₂+Δ₃(精确增量=一阶项+二阶交互项),监控主导一阶项Δ₂的奇异谱熵。使用压缩核方法(只需计算dₖ×dₖ的核心矩阵)避免构造d×d密集积,在Ascend 910B NPU上实现显著加速。

(2) MoE路由器监控:利用softmax的平移不变性,推导出条件比ε=‖wᵢ-w̄‖/‖w̄‖下界化权重相似度sim(W_R)≥1-(n/(n-1))ε²(命题3)。推导局部权重-熵联系:log n - E[H(p)] ≈ (1/2n)tr(W_R,cᵀM_x W_R,c)。分析学习率和GBS通过"稳定赢家"反馈回路对路由器的影响。

(3) 通过理论分析(LR放大相干边际增长、小batch增加边际噪声)和故障注入实验验证。

Result: 故障注入实验结果:
- 低精度FA故障下,Δ₂谱熵坍缩发生在~5,000步,ΔW熵坍缩发生在~13,000步,损失发散在~22,000步——最早指标领先损失约17,000步。
- 学习率和GBS扫描:较大学习率和较小批量一致降低路由器每token熵,移除辅助负载均衡损失时效应最强。
- 两种故障签名可分离:路由器指标对低精度注意力故障不敏感,ΔW谱对LR/GBS变化不敏感。
- 组合故障继承两种签名而不互相遮蔽。
- 压缩核方法在单Ascend 910B NPU上对hidden size≥200实现8.6×-50×加速,相对误差~10⁻⁷。

开放源代码MoE检查点的路由器权重相似度差异显著:GPT-OSS接近0,Qwen3-35B-A3B约0.513,操作点强依赖于架构。

Conclusion: 本文展示了"机制驱动监控"作为可迁移的设计原则:不是寻找通用监控架构,而是为每个模块从其故障机制推导出对应监控器。两个案例证明该原则的可行性——从模块的预期计算和故障最早期可观测位置出发,得出能在损失发散前数千步预警的内部信号。论文明确指出局限:双线性分解假设显式W_q、W_k参数化(不直接适用于MLA/GQA/MQA/DSA);故障覆盖范围有限;Δ₂与ΔW熵坍缩的8,000步差距缺乏闭式预测;路由器权重-熵联系的激活各向同性假设在RMSNorm下不严格成立。设计原则本身是本文的可迁移贡献——随着现代LLM架构引入新模块(持久记忆存储、流行约束残差、稀疏机制),每个模块都需要从其自身机制推导监控器。

Abstract: Frontier large language model training consumes massive accelerator fleets and long wall-clock computation, making stability failures costly when they occur. After a numerical or a hyperparameter fault has already destabilized the training dynamics, it may continue for thousands of steps while loss and gradient norms still appear normal. We study mechanism-driven detection of training instability by deriving internal monitors from the functional role of each critical module and from the earliest computational sites where failures are expected to produce measurable signatures. For low-precision flash attention, we monitor the spectral entropy of a QK bilinear decomposition, whose first-order term becomes abnormal before the loss fully collapses. For MoE routers, we derive indicators from their role in expert selection. Our fault-injection experiments on low-precision attention, large learning-rate, and combined faults show that these signals provide distinct signatures for different failures, triggering thousands of steps before loss divergence.

</details>


### [41] [Vision-Default, Prior-Override: Causal Mechanisms of Perception-Knowledge Conflict in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28273)
*Niclas Lietzow,Danielle Bitterman,Carsten Eickhoff,William Rudman,Michal Golovanevsky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了视觉语言模型(VLM)在视觉证据与存储的世界知识冲突时的内部因果机制。通过激活修补和组件消融实验,发现视觉 grounding 是默认路径,而先验 grounding 仅依赖于网络中后半部分2.5-4.8%的稀疏注意力头(因果必需),消融这些头可翻转68-96%的先验预测,但仅影响0.8-7.5%的视觉预测,呈现出强烈的不对称因果结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在感知-知识冲突下的行为已被广泛研究,但现有工作多为行为层面或相关性分析,缺乏组件级别的因果机制解释。作者旨在揭示VLM如何以及通过哪些内部组件解决视觉证据与先验知识之间的冲突,为提升多模态系统可靠性提供机理基础。

Method: 1) 数据集:使用Visual-Counterfact数据集(467个反事实颜色图像),对比原始与反事实图像在Visual和Prior两种prompt下的行为;2) 模型:五个模型覆盖三个VLM家族(Qwen-VL 3B/7B、LLaVA-NeXT 7B、PaliGemma 3B/10B);3) 激活修补:在三种粒度(残差流、注意力头、MLP子层)上进行P2V和V2P两个方向的修补,使用restoration score和flip rate度量;4) 组件消融:零消融促进头/抑制头,测试必要性;5) 机制分析:注意力模式分析(图像注意力分数)+ Logit Lens(头输出差异投影到词汇空间)。

Result: 1) 行为上,Prior grounding在冲突下准确率从86-96%骤降至17.7-55.7%,视觉 grounding 几乎不受影响(Δ≤7.3%);2) 决策定位在网络后半部分的临界窗口(52-100%深度,7-16层),V2P比P2V早3-19层达到50%翻转率;3) 仅2.5-4.8%的注意力头(8-24个)被分类为因果必需,集中在后半网络;4) 消融促进头可翻转68-96%的先验预测,但仅翻转0.8-7.5%的视觉预测,验证强不对称因果结构;5) MLP子层呈现同方向但弱1.2-8倍的不对称效应,起放大器作用而非主路由;6) 机制上,识别出早期routing heads(调节信息流)和晚期writing heads(直接将答案token投射到残差流);7) 跨架构差异:Qwen-VL和LLaVA-NeXT通过在图像/文本token间重分配注意力实现路由,PaliGemma通过改变被attend的表示实现路由。

Conclusion: VLM通过"视觉默认、先验覆盖"的非对称机制解决感知-知识冲突:视觉 grounding 默认为基线路径,而先验 grounding 需要由稀疏的(2.5-4.8%)、网络后半部分的因果必需注意力头主动注入。该电路可分解为早期routing heads和晚期writing heads,MLP起辅助放大作用。该机制在Qwen-VL、LLaVA-NeXT、PaliGemma三个架构家族中一致存在,但路由实现方式存在架构特异性(注意力重分配 vs 表示调制)。识别出的组件为可控多模态推理提供了具体干预目标。

Abstract: Vision-language models must reconcile visual evidence with memorized world knowledge when the two conflict. How they resolve this conflict shapes the reliability of multimodal systems, yet prior work characterizes it behaviorally without a component-level causal account. We combine activation patching across three granularities (residual stream, attention heads, and MLP sublayers) with model-component ablation studies and mechanistic analysis. Across three VLM families, we find that visual grounding emerges by default, whereas prior grounding depends on a small set of causally necessary attention heads (2.5-4.8%) concentrated in the second half of the network. These heads enable answers from stored world knowledge (e.g., "red" for a strawberry) despite conflicting visual input. Ablating them flips predictions from knowledge-grounded to visually grounded answers in 68-96% of cases under prior-knowledge prompts, but changes only 0.8-7.5% of visually grounded predictions, establishing an asymmetric causal structure. The identified heads decompose into routing heads, which modulate information flow, and writing heads, which directly project answer tokens into the residual stream. This structure is consistent across model families and scales, revealing a sparse causal circuit underlying perception-knowledge conflict in VLMs.

</details>

### [42] [SidConArena: An Environment Evaluating Agents in Open-Ended,Positive-Sum Bargaining Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27397)
*Yeqi Feng,Yuxin Chen,Tianxing He*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出 SidConArena，一个基于桌游《Sidereal Confluence》的多智能体基准测试框架，用于评估 LLM 智能体在开放式、正和（合作-竞争混合动机）议价博弈中的能力。该框架将经济交互形式化为有限时域部分可观察随机博弈（POSG），包含自然语言谈判、确定性生产转换器、密封式拍卖三个耦合阶段。实验表明更强的前沿模型经济表现更优，但智能体仍存在资源误估值、被动议价、长期投资规划不足等系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体评测基准多为静态推理或零和博弈（如 Diplomacy、狼人杀等），无法反映现实经济交互中的混合动机特性——智能体既需合作创造盈余，又需竞争稀缺资源，同时需处理延迟回报。SidConArena 旨在填补这一空白，提供一个能够评估议价、资源分配、长期投资规划等综合经济能力的开放环境。

Method: 1. **形式化建模**：将多玩家经济建模为有限时域 POSG，定义联合动作空间 𝒜 = 𝒜_neg ∪ 𝒜_bid ∪ 𝒜_prod，并设定终局价值函数 V_terminal。2. **三阶段耦合机制**：（a）谈判阶段——基于启发式效用 U(s) 的合作子博弈，仅在帕累托改进时执行绑定交易；（b）生产阶段——激活向量的确定性背包式组合优化；（c）拍卖阶段——多轨道密封式优先权拍卖，引入不完美信息。3. **系统架构**：分层 Brain 调度器按阶段路由任务至专用模块（TurnPlan/Trade/Economy/Bid/Pick Caller），通过神经-符号动作接口将自由推理转化为可验证的函数调用；采用客户端-服务器异步发布-订阅模式支持高频并发谈判。4. **观测设计**：四通道结构化观测（私有、公板、市场、交互），支持人机混合对弈。

Result: 1. **同质自博弈**：GPT-5、Gemini-3-Flash-Preview 获得最高终局分数，GPT-4o-mini 等轻量模型表现显著偏低；GLM-4.7 表现弱说明该基准不单纯复现模型版本时序。2. **异质 Elo 锦标赛**：前沿模型在混合对局中仍具竞争优势，但绝对收益与相对优势不完全一致——部分模型在异质对手面前可能因过度支付或策略僵化而丧失优势。3. **失败模式诊断**：（a）局部估值错误——智能体将 Ship（拍卖货币）的边际交换价值与战略重要性混淆，常以多单位资源换取单 Ship；（b）被动议价——在持有稀缺资源时接受首个合理报价，不利用多方竞争进行反报价或保留选择权；（c）短期规划失败——单步决策看似合理，但未能形成"早期投资→复合生产→终局转化"的一致轨迹。

Conclusion: SidConArena 验证了其作为交互式经济智能体评测基准的判别力，前沿模型的终局表现显著优于轻量模型，但轨迹分析揭示了当前 LLM 智能体与深层经济推理之间的根本差距：表面合规的指令遵循无法替代对情境估值、议价权力、机会成本、生产协同与延迟回报的综合推理。该基准有望推动具备稳健正和协调、战略性谈判与长期经济规划能力的下一代 LLM 智能体研究。局限性包括：未进行系统性人机混合评估、谈判协议不含非绑定承诺与信誉机制、未做环境特定微调、博弈规模有限、部分失败模式受特定资源系统影响。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM agents requires dynamic environments that go beyond static reasoning and zero-sum games. Real-world economic interaction is often open-ended and mixed-motive: agents must negotiate, create positive-sum surplus, compete for scarce assets, and plan under delayed returns. We introduce SidConArena, a new benchmark framework for evaluating LLM agents in open-ended, positive-sum bargaining. SidConArena formalizes a multi-player economy as a finite-horizon partially observable stochastic game with three coupled phases: natural-language negotiation with binding trades, deterministic converter-based production, and sealed-bid auctions for long-term assets. The framework combines structured observations, phase-aware agent dispatching, a neural-symbolic action interface, and asynchronous execution, enabling free-form interaction while preserving rule-grounded evaluation. Across homogeneous and heterogeneous tournaments, stronger frontier models achieve higher economic outcomes, yet agents still misvalue resources, bargain passively, and remain limited in long-horizon investment planning.

</details>


### [43] [Glite ARF: Verifier-Driven Research with Parallel LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27416)
*Vassili Philippov,Pavel Katunin,Dmitry Andreev,Igor Ostanin,Anton Nikolaev*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Glite ARF 是一个开源 Python 框架，通过"三层角色栈"（人类研究者 + LLM 编码代理 + 确定性 Python 验证脚本）实现可审计、可复现的并行 LLM 代理研究。其核心思想是"验证驱动的研究"——将研究流程的规则编码为会大声失败的脚本，而非依赖代理遵循的提示词。该框架在 BEA 2026 词汇难度共享任务中取得闭轨第一、开轨第二的成绩（129 个特征集、273 个任务、~450 美元 API 成本），并在 3 个领域、3 个多周活动中验证其结构化机制仅增加约 1% 的墙钟时间开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 编码代理让自动化实证研究看似唾手可得，但朴素委派无法扩展到大型项目：代理对指令的低频遗漏会复合成损坏的、不可复现的产物。例如 The AI Scientist v1 生成实验中 42% 因编码错误而失败；作者自身审计的 BEA 2026 项目中也出现了 13 起类似事故（如一个代理步骤本应追加 2244 行测试数据，却重算了 38 个特征集的完整特征，污染了 20304 行历史训练数据；又如四个特征集将目标变量泄露进 LLM 提示）。纯靠优化提示词无法让失败率收敛到零，因此需要将研究流程的规则编码为确定性的、强制执行的代码。

Method: 框架定义三层角色栈：(1) 人类研究者提出假设（角色 1，明确保持人类驱动）；(2) 编码代理（Claude Code、Codex CLI）在固定结构下执行任务（角色 2），每个任务对应独立文件夹、独立 git worktree、独立分支、独立 PR；(3) 确定性 Python 验证脚本（verificators）强制执行任务隔离、已完成任务不可变性、修正叠加层和物化的项目概览（角色 3）。七项结构原则包括：任务隔离、不可变性 + 修正叠加层、仅通过聚合器跨任务读取、物化项目概览、规格验证的产物、全面日志、子代理隔离。每个产物有版本化规范（specification）和对应的验证脚本，错误码（如 PM-E003）阻断提交与合并；修正通过下游任务文件夹内的 correction 文件在读取时叠加应用，原始记录不被改写。复杂任务拆为研究→规划→实现→分析→报告等子代理链，各有独立上下文。任务并行运行于同一 48GB Mac 笔记本，最多 12 个会话同时执行。

Result: BEA 2026 词汇难度共享任务（西、德、中三种 L1）：闭轨三项第一、开轨三项第二；平均 RMSE 较官方基线降低 29.9%（闭轨）和 35.9%（开轨）。最佳单组件为 LLaMA-3.1-8B LoRA 回归头，K 折 RMSE 0.831（0.13% 可训练参数）。活动规模 273 个跟踪任务、146 次实验运行、129 个特征集、1161 个数值特征列；最高 12 个代理并行；总成本 $498.31（LLM API $449.69 + 租用 A100 $48.62）。最具说明性的成果是发现并隔离了 4 个目标泄露特征集（v13 RMSE 0.609 → v14 RMSE 0.802，修正为合理值）。在另外两个领域（词义消歧 WSD 167 任务、CEFR 可读性 35 任务）共 3 个多周活动中，框架自身机制仅占约 25% 的命令数和约 1% 的墙钟时间；305 次合并中无任务间数据污染或主分支冲突；框架代码在三个项目中字节级相同。

Conclusion: 验证驱动的研究以极小的时间成本（~1%）换取了结构性错误的可检测性与可审计性。代理规则应编码在确定性脚本中，而非让代理"承诺"遵循散文式说明。但验证器有明确边界：它们捕获结构性错误（文件越界、日志缺失、JSON 不合规、分数不可溯源），但不评判语义正确性（实验设计是否合理）；语义判断仍属人类。角色 1（假设选择）按设计保持人类驱动，理由是当前前沿模型在多周尺度上不可靠地执行研究方向选择。框架假设单一操作者、面对非对抗性代理漂移（不防恶意代理主动改写验证器），并已开源（Apache 2.0）附带公开演示项目。

Abstract: LLM coding agents make it tempting to automate empirical research by delegating experiments to them directly, but naive delegation does not scale to large projects: low-rate instruction lapses compound into broken, irreproducible artefacts. To address this problem, we present Glite ARF, an open-source Python framework for running many LLM coding agents in parallel on a research repository without sacrificing reproducibility or auditability. The framework defines a three-role stack: a human researcher chooses which hypotheses to test, coding agents (Claude Code, Codex CLI) implement individual tasks under a fixed structure, and deterministic Python verifier scripts enforce task isolation, immutability of completed work, a corrections overlay, and a materialised project overview. We call this verifier-driven research: the rules of the research process live in code that fails loudly when violated, not in prose that agents are merely asked to follow. Using Glite ARF, we developed our submission to the BEA 2026 vocabulary-difficulty shared task, placing first in the closed track and second in the open track on all three target languages (Spanish, German, Mandarin) and reducing the official baseline RMSE by 29.9% (closed) and 35.9% (open). The campaign comprised 273 tracked tasks (146 experiment runs) across 129 feature sets, run by up to twelve parallel agents orchestrated from a single laptop - with some model training on rented A100s - at approximately \$450 in LLM API spend (\$498 total third-party cost), and structured per-fold provenance let us catch and strip four target-leaking feature sets, correcting an implausible 0.609 RMSE to 0.802. Across three campaigns in three domains, the framework's structural machinery adds only about 1% of wall-clock time. Framework and a public demo project accompany this paper.

</details>


### [44] [Delayed Verification Destabilizes Multi-Agent LLM Belief: Instability Thresholds and Optimal Corrector Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27409)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体LLM系统中延迟验证的稳定性悖论：验证延迟与强度本身可能将事实性共识转化为振荡。核心发现是验证存在一个封闭形式的剂量-延迟稳定阈值（δ=2时阈值为黄金比例倒数≈0.618），同步通信与验证延迟是最差工况；通过接地拉普拉斯谱分解与超模性分析，给出(1-1/e)近最优的贪心校正器布置规则；并在五个开源模型上实证验证了符号信念任务中的Hopf振荡签名，而接地事实问答因真理作为吸收边界而保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统中幻觉从单模型的静态属性演变为智能体间的动态级联过程；现有标准缓解方案（添加验证器/批评智能体）因验证存在内在延迟，而延迟负反馈在控制论中已知会导致振荡与不稳定。现有LLM智能体文献缺乏对验证闭环动态稳定性的建模与分析，也缺少剂量-延迟权衡的定量刻画；既有的DeGroot共识、隐藏锚定审议及单智能体自纠错模型均无延迟或仅限单智能体，无法解释多智能体辩论退化的动力学机制。

Method: 将多智能体审议建模为带接地校正器节点的图上延迟共识，通过对称拉普拉斯L的接地主子矩阵L_g进行谱分解，将n_f维延迟网络解耦为独立标量延迟递推x_{t+1}=a_i·x_t - ηκ·x_{t-δ} + ηĝ_i。利用Jury稳定性条件、Kuruklis离散延迟稳定性理论、切比雪夫多项式单调性及D-分解方法，推导封闭形式的剂量阈值β_c(a,δ)=sinθ/sin(δθ)及两延迟联合下的三项式稳定性边界；对接地拉普拉斯+对角钉扎矩阵的M-矩阵结构，证明相干性H(R)=tr M(R)^{-1}的超模性，从而由Nemhauser-Wolsey-Fisher定理给出(1-1/e)近最优的贪心校正器布置保证；在五个开源模型上进行合成验证与真实LLM辩论实证（数值估计与接地事实QA两类任务对比）。

Result: （1）稳定性阈值：δ=1时系统良性（ηκ<1即可稳定）；δ≥2时剂量上限β_c单调下降，δ=2时β_c(1,2)=(√5-1)/2≈0.618；通信与验证延迟同步（d=δ）为最不稳定工况。（2）布置理论：接地通过提升μ_min(L_g)放松剂量限制，贪心算法按Sherman-Morrison边际增益放置于放大器/桥接节点，在3×5-团+桥接图上k=8时残差误差从9.1降至2.3，优于度优先(2.9)与随机(2.5)。（3）实证：合成tanh饱和系统振荡起始点与理论预测吻合（误差约2%）；真实LLM数值估计辩论中(α=0.5, δ=6)工况下符号过零率达96–100%，而(α=0.5, δ=1)工况下仅0–4%，跨Qwen3.6-35B/14B、Mistral-7B、Phi-4、Gemma-4-12B五个模型均呈现8/8问题的方向一致性（单侧Wilcoxon p=0.004，经Bonferroni校正仍显著）。（4）接地事实QA在δ∈{0,1,6}扫描中振幅不随δ增长，真理作为吸收边界消除了延迟诱导振荡。

Conclusion: 本文将多智能体LLM验证回路建模为图上延迟共识，导出封闭形式阈值揭示验证具有稳定性预算：过强或过迟的校正会将事实共识反转为振荡。最差工况为通信与验证延迟同步，剂量上限退化至黄金比例倒数。有限的校正器预算应以贪心方式优先部署于高影响（放大器/桥接）节点。实证确认理论预测，并界定其适用范围——延迟诱导振荡为符号信念任务所固有，而接地事实验证因真理的吸收边界而保持稳定。该结论颠覆了"验证越多越好"的默认认知：验证存在最优剂量与最优位置，约束瓶颈为最慢接地模式，可通过合理布置加以放松。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems often rely on verifier and critic agents to suppress hallucinations, but verification is delayed. During this delay, false claims can propagate through the agent network. We model this process as delayed consensus on a graph with grounded corrector nodes. Spectral decomposition by the grounded Laplacian yields a closed-form stability threshold for the verification dose: correction that is too strong or too delayed can turn consensus into oscillation. The most unstable regime occurs when the communication and verification delays coincide; for delay two, the threshold is the inverse golden ratio. The same framework gives a supermodular placement objective and a greedy (1-1/e)-approximation rule for assigning a limited corrector budget to influential nodes. Experiments across five open models confirm the predicted dose-delay oscillations. By contrast, grounded factual answering makes truth an absorbing boundary and eliminates the effect, suggesting that the instability is specific to signed-belief tasks while grounded verification remains stabilizing

</details>


### [45] [QueenBee Planner: Skill-Evolving Communication Topologies for Token-Efficient LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27492)
*Congjia Tian,Yuhang Yao,Jiaming Cui*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: QueenBee Planner提出了一种将多智能体系统中的通信拓扑作为可检索、自进化的设计技能的框架。该方法冻结工作智能体池,通过外部LLM规划器学习生成时序通信DAG,执行轨迹被提炼为带证据的设计规则(Preserve/Modify/Avoid三类动作)。在Count-Frequency聚合和Silo-Bench分布式协调任务上的实验表明,自进化的图生成在RMSE、通信成本、token消耗等方面均优于固定拓扑和冷启动基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统通常为某一任务设计固定拓扑后即停止优化,未能积累可复用的通信结构设计知识。与此同时,自反思类系统积累的是任务执行经验而非通信结构设计经验。该论文旨在让多智能体系统的"架构师"(规划器)在其生命周期内持续学习如何更好地组织智能体间的通信结构。核心挑战在于如何防止自进化循环将噪声(幸运运行或不可靠的LLM解释)固化为策略。

Method: 方法核心包含四个机制:(1)规划器-工作器分离:冻结工作智能体、任务适配器和评分函数,仅让外部LLM规划器学习生成时序通信DAG G=(N,T,E,h,I,R),指定谁向谁发送信息、合并者、最终答案持有者及合并规则;(2)基于图块的结构化信用分配:将生成图分解为8种标签不变的结构图块(round数、消息数、最大扇入、扇入桶、汇点存在、汇点数、审计边、归约深度),避免信用只绑定完整拓扑名;(3)技能进化:三类更新动作(正例规则、反例规则、可执行scaffold),并通过hold-out接受门控、方差感知信用、图块级归因、迁移信任、洞见证伪、结构去重六层约束确保更新可信;(4)自进化循环算法:在训练任务上生成候选DAG,收集证据,经噪声过滤和证伪后,由hold-out门控决定新规则是否进入技能库。

Result: 在Count-Frequency fulltest设置(DeepSeek-V4-Flash,N=8,数组长度1024)上,最佳自进化图(hybrid reduce-audit)将RMSE从最强固定拓扑(one-peer exponential DAG star)的12.53降至7.87,同时消息数减少74.2%(31→8)、模型调用减少54.5%、token成本减少约59.6%。跨模型(GPT-4o、Qwen系列、DeepSeek系列、MiMo)的成对对比均显示自进化方法一致优于固定拓扑(GPT-4o iter5提升56.1%,Qwen3.5-Flash提升29.9%)。在Silo-Bench风格任务上,三轮自进化后准确率达到0.539 EM,高于最佳单一固定拓扑(0.333)和per-case oracle固定拓扑(0.417),表明技能库学到了可迁移的、超越固定拓扑家族的细粒度条件化结构。

Conclusion: 论文证明多智能体系统的通信结构本身可以作为显式学习对象,而非实现细节。QueenBee Planner通过严格的hold-out门控和结构化信用机制,使得自进化的设计循环可以在不断积累Preserve/Modify/Avoid技能的同时避免过拟合和噪声固化。在精确可验证的聚合任务和多样化的分布式协调任务上的双重验证表明,系统学习的是可迁移的架构设计知识(关于何时保留、修改或避免通信模式),而非任务答案记忆。未来工作应扩展到更大任务套件、更长进化周期,并分析哪些结构图块对准确性、成本和鲁棒性贡献最稳定。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) multi-agent systems increasingly depend not only on how individual agents reason, but also on how agents are connected. This paper introduces QueenBee Planner, a framework that treats inter-agent communication topology as a retrievable and self-improving design skill. A pool of worker agents, the task adapter, and the scoring function are frozen; only an outer LLM planner learns to generate temporal communication DAGs specifying who sends information to whom, in which round, who merges messages, and who emits the final answer. Execution traces are distilled into evidence-backed design rules with three actions: \emph{Preserve}, \emph{Modify}, and \emph{Avoid}. To prevent self-evolution from turning lucky runs or plausible but false explanations into policy, QueenBee uses held-out acceptance gates, variance-aware credit, motif-level attribution, transfer trust, insight falsification, and structural deduplication. We evaluate the method on Count-Frequency aggregation and Silo-Bench-style distributed coordination tasks. With fixed workers, self-evolved graph generation produces communication structures that improve over fixed topologies and cold generation. In the CF fulltest setting, the best generated graph reduces RMSE from 12.53 for the strongest fixed topology to 7.87 while also reducing messages, model calls, and token cost; Silo-style results show the same direction of improvement over cold and fixed-topology baselines. These results suggest that multi-agent systems can learn reusable architectural design knowledge rather than merely memorizing task answers.

</details>


### [46] [GenWorld: Empirically Grounded Urban Simulation Infrastructure for Scalable LLM-Agent Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27650)
*Gen Li,Jieyuan Lan,Pengcheng Xu,Zongyuan Wu,Masaki Ogura,Tao Feng*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: GenWorld提出了一种基于经验数据支撑的城市级LLM智能体仿真基础设施，通过建筑级合成城市、结构化智能体-环境接口以及将LLM决策信号离线编译为查找策略来解决联合接地(grounding)与可扩展性(scaling)问题，并以日本东广岛为参考实例进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体仿真面临两大核心挑战：一是智能体行为需扎根于真实城市环境约束（接地问题），二是直接在线调用LLM进行城市级大规模人口仿真计算成本过高（扩展性问题）。论文旨在解决这两个联合难题，为基于LLM的城市级智能体研究提供可复现、可扩展的基础平台。

Method: 1. 构建建筑级合成城市（基于普查与地理空间数据）；2. 设计结构化的智能体-环境交互接口；3. 采用离线编译策略，将LLM推导的决策信号预编译为查找策略（lookup policies），避免在线推理开销；4. 使用YJMob100K手机信令数据作为通勤距离诊断验证；5. 通过三个可复现案例进行验证：全城工作日推演、工作日-周末行为对比、警告响应扰动与可审计重规划。

Result: 在日本东广岛的参考实例中，GenWorld成功接地了196,608名合成居民，其人口统计一致性通过普查数据表验证，通勤距离分布通过YJMob100K手机数据诊断校验。三个案例（全城工作日推演、周内-周末对比、警告响应扰动）均展示了平台的可复现性与可审计性，验证了基础设施在大规模LLM智能体仿真中的可行性。

Conclusion: GenWorld可作为扎根于真实数据且可扩展的LLM智能体研究的可复现平台；但论文明确指出，针对交通疏散、应急疏散或政策效果的校准预测仍是未来工作，平台当前定位为基础仿真基础设施而非预测工具。

Abstract: LLM-agent simulation faces a joint grounding and scaling problem: agents should act in environments that reflect real urban constraints, yet direct online LLM calls for city-scale populations are computationally prohibitive. We present GenWorld, an empirically grounded urban simulation infrastructure that combines a building-level synthetic city, a structured agent-environment interface, and offline compilation of LLM-derived decision signals into lookup policies for scalable rollout. In a reference instantiation for Higashihiroshima, Japan, GenWorld grounds 196,608 synthetic residents in census and geospatial data, validates demographic consistency against census tabulations, and uses YJMob100K mobile-phone data as a commuting-distance diagnostic. We demonstrate the infrastructure through three reproducible cases: a full-city weekday rollout, a weekday-weekend behavioral contrast, and a warning-response perturbation with auditable replanning traces. These cases support GenWorld as a reproducible platform for grounded and scalable LLM-agent studies, while calibrated forecasting for traffic, evacuation, or policy outcomes remains future work.

</details>


### [47] [GBC: Gradient-Based Connections for Optimizing Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28187)
*Xiaocheng Yang,Abdulrahman Alrabah,Dilek Hakkani-Tür,Gokhan Tur*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出梯度连接（GBC）方法，通过将多智能体系统建模为计算图并引入基于梯度的token级连接权重，实现对多智能体系统（MAS）的细粒度归因与提示优化。基于此开发的AgentChord框架通过前缀式梯度计算降低内存开销，在MultiWOZ和τ-bench上的实验表明GBC能显著提升多智能体性能并超越强单智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的多智能体系统（MAS）在任务分解和角色专业化方面具有潜力，但实际表现常受限于智能体间的失调与协调问题。其根本挑战在于缺乏细粒度的信用分配机制：现有方法仅依赖粗粒度反馈（如整体任务成败），难以精确定位哪个智能体或交互环节导致了错误，从而限制了系统调试与自动优化的有效性。

Method: GBC方法将MAS建模为有向无环计算图G=(V,E)，每个智能体由提示-模型对(P,M)定义。其核心包含四个组件：1) 智能体图：按拓扑序执行智能体；2) 梯度连接：定义四种token级连接权重——L1范数的均值/最大值、梯度-输入乘积的均值/最大值，用于量化前置输出对当前输出的影响；3) 任务特定的文本损失函数（verbal loss）；4) 优化器。通过构建归因图G_attr并保留top-m前驱节点，反向传播损失提取归因轨迹，再以LLM作为优化器迭代更新提示。AgentChord采用前缀式梯度计算策略——先无梯度前向传播提示获取KV缓存，再对输入部分启用梯度计算，将内存复杂度从O(n·d·L)降至O((n-k)·d·L)。

Result: 在MultiWOZ 2.4上：优化前多智能体系统并不稳定优于单智能体基线；经GBC优化后，Qwen-3-32B采用L1范数均值变体取得最佳表现，JGA从28.9提升至54.4，Slot F1从79.3提升至91.4，Inform达99.0，Success达94.0。在τ-bench上：Qwen-3-32B多智能体系统经L1范数最大值优化后，整体奖励从13.0提升至24.3，超越强单智能体基线（22.6）。L1范数变体在归因准确性和优化效果上均表现最佳，验证了归因质量与优化有效性正相关。错误分析显示MultiWOZ主要存在跨域错误和信息遗漏问题，τ-bench则以检索/识别失败为主。运行时间方面，Qwen-3-32B在MultiWOZ上约8-9.5小时，τ-bench上约5-6小时。

Conclusion: GBC是首个面向多智能体系统的token级跨智能体信用分配框架，通过梯度连接实现精确的错误归因和定向的提示优化。AgentChord框架通过前缀式梯度计算使该方法具有可扩展性。实验证明GBC能持续提升多智能体性能，并在多数情况下使其匹配或超越强单智能体基线，同时揭示归因质量与优化效果正相关。但该方法存在计算成本较高、依赖verbal loss设计质量、仅为一阶近似等局限性。未来可探索更高效的梯度近似、与强化学习结合及动态多智能体拓扑扩展。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models (LLMs) provide a promising framework for solving complex tasks through role specialization and structured interaction. However, their performance is often limited by miscoordination and, more fundamentally, the lack of fine-grained credit assignment across agents. Existing approaches typically rely on coarse-grained feedback, making it difficult to identify which agents or interaction steps are responsible for errors. We propose Gradient-Based Connections (GBC), an approach for fine-grained attribution and optimization of multi-agent systems. GBC models a MAS as a computational graph and introduces gradient-based connection weights to quantify the influence of each agent's output on downstream agents at the token level. By constructing an attribution graph and propagating task-specific loss signals backward, our method enables precise identification of error sources and targeted prompt optimization. We further develop AgentChord, an efficient implementation that leverages prefix-based gradient computation. Experiments on MultiWOZ and {\tau}-bench show that GBC improves multi-agent performance and outperforms strong single-agent and multi-agent baselines, and higher attribution quality is associated with greater optimization effectiveness. Code is available at: this https URL .

</details>

### [48] [DataStates-LLM: Scalable Checkpointing for Transformer Models Using Composable State Providers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16956)
*Avinash Maurya,M. Mustafa Rafique,Franck Cappello,Bogdan Nicolae*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DataStates-LLM 是一种面向超大规模 Transformer 模型（最高 70B 参数，256 块 A100 GPU）的高性能异步检查点系统。它通过引入"可组合状态提供者（State Providers）"将状态抽象与数据移动解耦，并利用前向/反向传播中参数的不可变性实现"惰性"非阻塞快照，将检查点吞吐量最高提升 4 倍，端到端训练时间最高缩短 2.2 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 训练已扩展到数千块 GPU、持续数周乃至数月，硬件故障不可避免（如 Llama 3 405B 训练平均每 2.8 小时故障一次）。检查点对容错、挂起恢复、回滚调试、RLHF、迁移学习等场景至关重要。然而现有检查点方案将模型状态视为不透明二进制块，忽略了其"3D 异构性"——内存位置（GPU/Host）、分片数量、数据类型（张量/Python 对象）及序列化需求的差异——导致设备到主机的阻塞传输、数据无关的序列化以及存储 I/O 争用，开销巨大。

Method: 1. **可组合状态提供者（State Providers）**：轻量级中间件抽象，封装异构数据结构语义，为数据移动引擎提供统一的流式视图；对张量实现零拷贝序列化，对 Python 对象按需序列化。2. **惰性非阻塞捕获**：利用前向/反向传播中参数和优化器状态的不可变性，在 GPU→Host 拷贝与计算重叠，仅在更新阶段前等待一致性同步。3. **分片合并与预分配**：预先分配并预钉扎主机内存池，合并各分片拷贝，消除分配开销和等待刷新。4. **多级流水线 I/O 引擎**：采用 C++/CUDA 实现，使用 liburing 与 O_DIRECT 加速持久化 I/O，专用 CUDA 流执行 DMA 传输，后台线程负责主机到存储的刷新。5. **混合固定偏移 + 并发日志追加布局**：将 I/O 与元数据序列化重叠，先写入已知大小的张量块，再并发追加序列化对象，最后追加元数据头。6. **与 DeepSpeed 集成**：作为 drop-in 后端替换默认 torch.save 引擎，无需修改模型代码。

Result: 在 ALCF Polaris 超级计算机上对 BLOOM 3B 及 Llama 7B/13B/33B/70B 模型进行评估：①检查点吞吐量较 DeepSpeed/TorchSnapshot 提升 2–10 倍，较前作 DataStates-LLM-Old 提升 1.2–7 倍；②端到端训练时间缩短 1.3–2.2 倍（最高 2.2 倍）；③在 7B 模型不同数据并行度下缩短 1.3–5.7 倍；④支持 5 倍更频繁的检查点而开销与基线相当；⑤元数据/序列化时间从 3.9 秒（DeepSpeed）降至 0.0156 秒；⑥主机到文件刷新较 TorchSnapshot 提升 1.25–2.5 倍；⑦在 256 块 A100 GPU 上针对 70B 模型保持近乎均匀的有效吞吐量。

Conclusion: 现有 LLM 专用检查点引擎仍未充分利用迭代语义（参数在前向/反向阶段的不可变性）来安全地重叠暂存与持久化，且因同步的、数据无关的序列化及粗粒度刷新造成 PCIe/内存/存储带宽未充分使用。DataStates-LLM 通过分片合并的 GPU→Host 暂存、惰性非阻塞快照、流式多级持久化、层次化异构状态提供者以及 I/O 与元数据序列化的深度重叠，显著缓解了序列化与异构性瓶颈。未来工作将引入差分检查点与压缩以进一步降低高频率下的网络/存储开销，并探索分片聚合以缓解并行文件系统的元数据压力。

Abstract: The rapid growth of Large Transformer-based models, specifically Large Language Models (LLMs), now scaling to trillions of parameters, has necessitated training across thousands of GPUs using complex hybrid parallelism strategies (e.g., data, tensor, and pipeline parallelism). Checkpointing this massive, distributed state is critical for a wide range of use cases, such as resilience, suspend-resume, investigating undesirable training trajectories, and explaining model evolution. However, existing checkpointing solutions typically treat model state as opaque binary blobs, ignoring the ``3D heterogeneity'' of the underlying data structures--varying by memory location (GPU vs. Host), number of ``logical'' objects sharded and split across multiple files, data types (tensors vs. Python objects), and their serialization requirements. This results in significant runtime overheads due to blocking device-to-host transfers, data-oblivious serialization, and storage I/O contention. In this paper, we introduce DataStates-LLM, a novel checkpointing architecture that leverages State Providers to decouple state abstraction from data movement. DataStates-LLM exploits the immutability of model parameters during the forward and backward passes to perform ``lazy'', non-blocking asynchronous snapshots. By introducing State Providers, we efficiently coalesce fragmented, heterogeneous shards and overlap the serialization of metadata with bulk tensor I/O. We evaluate DataStates-LLM on models up to 70B parameters on 256 A100-40GB GPUs. Our results demonstrate that DataStates-LLM achieves up to 4$\times$ higher checkpointing throughput and reduces end-to-end training time by up to 2.2$\times$ compared to state-of-the-art solutions, effectively mitigating the serialization and heterogeneity bottlenecks in extreme-scale LLM training.

</details>


### [49] [Optimizing Teacher-Student Partitioning for Scalable Knowledge Distillation on HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27797)
*Adrian P. Dieguez,Victor Conchello Vendrell,Alex Batlle,Vinnam Kim,Jordi Ros-Giralt,Harris Teague*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种HPC感知的知识蒸馏方法，通过解耦教师模型与学生模型的分区策略，利用两者在内存占用和通信需求上的显著不对称性，在TRL的GKD实现基础上实现最高67%的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TRL库中实现的GKD（Generalized Knowledge Distillation）方法对称地处理教师模型和学生模型，将教师模型通过DeepSpeed训练引擎执行，导致不必要的优化器状态分配；同时强制两者使用相同的分区策略，忽略了教师仅需推理而学生需训练的本质差异，浪费了GPU内存并限制了吞吐量。

Method: 1. 分析TRL GKD的多节点低效问题，识别优化器状态过度分配和对称分区的两大瓶颈；2. 修改教师初始化代码，移除不必要的训练状态（优化器设为None或轻量优化器），实现ZeRO-2级别的分片；3. 用DeepSpeed-Inference引擎（水平张量并行TP）替代训练引擎处理教师模型；4. 构建解析性能模型，对比垂直分区（DDP）和水平分区（TP）的通信-内存权衡，推导拐点存在的不等式条件；5. 在16节点×8×H100集群上使用Llama-3 8B（量化学生+FP教师）进行实证验证。

Result: 1. 相比TRL原始GKD实现，在16-128 GPU多节点场景中吞吐量提升最高67%，典型约50%；2. 微批次大小可显著增大（如4节点上从4提升到6）；3. 单节点场景下，水平（TP）教师分区优于垂直（DDP）教师分区（如μBS=3时TP达26.99 samples/s，而DDP变体要么OOM要么仅约20 samples/s）；4. 解析模型正确预测了NVLink等高带宽低延迟互连下TP分区更有利的拐点条件；5. 训练损失曲线与TRL基线一致，无精度损失。

Conclusion: 通过解耦师生分区、利用拓扑感知的混合并行策略（教师水平TP+学生垂直DDP），GKD训练可在不修改算法目标的前提下获得显著加速；该方案以非侵入式方式集成到现有TRL工作流中；未来工作包括将成本模型整合到自动调优、扩展到序列/流水线并行、处理长上下文和更大教师模型等。

Abstract: Knowledge Distillation (KD) enables training smaller student models under the guidance of larger teacher models, and the widely adopted TRL library implements it. Yet, TRL treats both models symmetrically, missing opportunities to exploit their pronounced asymmetry in memory footprint, and communication requirements. This paper presents an HPC-aware methodology for KD that decouples teacher and student partitioning efficiently. Our approach achieves up to 67% higher samples-per-second than TRL by avoiding unnecessary teacher-model data structures and selecting the best split strategy. We combine vertical and horizontal partitioning of models, deriving an analytical expression that identifies the existence of inflection points between splitting regimes. These results showed that exploiting teacher--student asymmetry through topology-aware parallelism notably accelerated GKD training on production HPC clusters at our company

</details>


### [50] [How far does a random forest generalize from a 54-run LAMMPS+SPICA benchmark?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27695)
*Dennis Alves Pedersen,Paulo Henrique Leme Ramalho,Fábio Andrijauskas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究利用基于冷启动的随机森林代理模型，仅需少量结构化基准数据训练，即可预测分子动力学（LAMMPS+SPICA）在HPC集群上的混合MPI+OpenMP配置性能，从而避免高成本的穷举式基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上为分子动力学工作负载在现代HPC集群上选择近最优的混合MPI+OpenMP配置，需要消耗大量计算资源进行穷举式经验基准测试，分配预算与评估的配置数量成正比。本文旨在探索是否可以用一个冷启动代理模型来预测性能并推荐高性能配置，从而大幅降低基准测试成本。

Method: 训练数据集由54次LAMMPS+SPICA运行组成，针对含有4354个粗粒化珠子的水合DOPC双分子层上的抗菌肽Tritrpticin问题，涵盖Lovelace集群（CENAPAD-SP）上1-8个AMD EPYC 7662节点的18种混合配置，每种配置有3次独立重复。九个拓扑和资源特征输入五个回归器，预测循环时间以及四个LAMMPS内部时间分项（Pair、Kspace、Comm、Modify）。通过特征重要性分析和留一维度（leave-one-dimension-out）泛化实验评估模型。

Result: 循环时间的样本内平均绝对误差为0.49秒（相对误差4.0%）。特征重要性分析显示预测信号主要来自拓扑变量（OpenMP线程数和MPI/OpenMP比例占主导；原始节点和核心数贡献低于3%）。留一维度泛化实验表明，精度取决于硬件机制归属：在共同机制内（单节点、多节点或共享线程层级），代理模型能正确对配置进行排序；但当目标跨越架构边界时，精度会下降。

Conclusion: 该研究产出了一个可解释的代理模型可信度映射图，明确指出了代理模型推荐可被信赖的应用场景，为以基准测试活动一小部分成本进行规划提供了实用价值。

Abstract: Selecting near-optimal hybrid MPI+OpenMP configurations for molecular dynamics workloads on modern HPC clusters has traditionally required exhaustive empirical benchmarking, consuming allocation budget proportional to the number of configurations evaluated. This work investigates whether a cold-start Random Forest surrogate, trained once on a small, structured benchmark dataset, can reliably predict execution performance and recommend high-performing configurations without further cluster runs. The training dataset comprises 54 LAMMPS+SPICA runs of the antimicrobial peptide Tritrpticin on a hydrated DOPC bilayer (4 354 coarse-grained beads), spanning 18 hybrid configurations on 1-8 AMD EPYC 7662 nodes of the Lovelace cluster at CENAPAD-SP, with three independent replications each. Nine topology and resource features feed five regressors that predict loop time and four internal LAMMPS timing fractions (Pair, Kspace, Comm, Modify). In-sample mean absolute error is 0.49 s on loop time (4.0 % relative). Feature importance localizes predictive signal in topology variables (OpenMP threads and MPI/OpenMP ratio dominate; raw node and core counts contribute under 3 %). Leave-one-dimension-out generalization reveals that accuracy is governed by hardware regime membership: within a common regime (single-node, multi-node, or shared threading tier) the surrogate ranks configurations correctly, and degrades when targets cross architectural boundaries. The result is an interpretable map of where the surrogate's recommendations can be trusted, useful for scoping further benchmark campaigns at a fraction of their nominal cost.

</details>


### [51] [Lightweight Multi-Vehicle Collaborative Perception Acceleration with Fusion Position Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27750)
*Wenzhao Zhang,Shujun Han,Haixiao Gao,Mengying Sun,Bizhu Wang,Xiaodong Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对多车协作感知（MvCP）在资源受限场景下的实时性挑战，提出了一种基于融合位置调整（FALL）的轻量级加速方案。该方案通过理论分析证明在DNN线性层间调整特征融合位置不会损失感知精度，并能在通信或计算资源受限时动态选择最优融合位置，实现低延迟协作感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶场景中，多车协作感知（MvCP）虽然能通过V2V通信弥补单车感知的局限性（遮挡、稀疏观测等问题），但其部署面临两大挑战：1）ITS环境中动态变化的无线信道质量和边缘设备有限的计算资源使得实时部署困难；2）现有加速方法（数据压缩、剪枝、量化等）通常以牺牲感知精度为代价，且难以同时适应计算受限和通信受限两种场景。因此需要一种既能保持精度又能在不同资源限制下灵活加速的方案。

Method: 本文提出FALL（FPe adjustment among Linear Layers）方案，核心方法包括三部分：（1）**条件可加性定义与分析**：为DNN推理放松"可加性"定义，提出"条件可加性"概念——函数F满足F(Σx_i)=ΣF_i(x_i)，并证明全连接层、卷积层、反卷积层、批归一化层、平均池化层均满足条件可加性，同时通过定理证明条件可加函数的复合与线性组合仍满足条件可加性；（2）**融合位置精度一致性证明**：基于条件可加性，分别推导前向和后向融合位置调整的精度一致性定理（定理3、4），并通过PIXOR模型实验验证（最大误差<0.05）；（3）**动态融合位置选择策略**：理论分析表明——前向位置（更靠前）可降低计算延迟但增加传输延迟，后向位置（更靠后）可降低传输延迟但增加计算延迟，因此需根据瓶颈资源（计算/通信）动态选择最优融合位置。

Result: 基于PIXOR模型的仿真结果显示FALL方案具有显著加速效果：（1）当通信资源受限时（R=5×10^8 bps），最优融合位置为后向b_5，可降低总延迟最高达**74.8%**；（2）当计算资源受限时（R=5×10^10 bps），最优融合位置为前向f_3，可降低总延迟最高达**30.3%**；（3）当计算与通信资源相对均衡时（R=5×10^9 bps），最优位置在原始FP附近，可降低延迟**23.6%**。此外，实验验证了不同融合位置下精度保持一致（在IoU=0.3/0.5/0.7下AP最大偏差不超过0.05），证实了理论分析的精度一致性。

Conclusion: 本文成功为基于加性特征融合的中间融合式多车协作感知（IF-MvCP）提出了一种轻量级加速方案FALL。该方案通过严格的理论分析（条件可加性及精度一致性定理）和基于PIXOR模型的仿真验证，证明其在不损失感知精度的前提下，能够通过动态调整特征融合位置有效降低端到端延迟。FALL方案具有同时适应计算受限和通信受限场景的灵活性，为边缘智能赋能的智能交通系统中实时MvCP部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-vehicle collaborative perception (MvCP) is considered as a key technology to facilitate automated driving (AD), where real-time MvCP under limited resources is significant for reliable AD. In this paper, we formulate a lightweight acceleration scheme for intermediate-fusion (IF) MvCP, which can adapt to both situations of limited computation and communication resources. We provide a relaxed definition conditional additivity and analyze the conditional additivity for various DNN linear layers. On this basis, we focus on the IF-MvCP based on additive feature fusion, and derive the MvCP precision consistency of the forward and backward feature fusion position (FP) adjustments among linear layers. Through experiments, we further validate the precision consistency of the FP adjustment method. Moreover, we propose an FP adjustment among linear layers (FALL) scheme for MvCP acceleration without precision loss theoretically. Simulation results show that the proposed FALL can reduce MvCP latency by up to 74.8% under limited communication resources and by up to 30.3% under limited computation resources.

</details>


### [52] [RAMSES: Secure high-performance computing for sensitive data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27919)
*Peter Heger,Lech Nieroda,Roland Pabel,Christoph Stollwerk,Stefan Borowski,Kamil Tokmakov,Michael Commer,Martin Peifer,Stefan Wesner,Viktor Achter*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RAMSES 是科隆大学 IT Center 设计的 HPC 系统，通过整合 AMD SEV 内存加密、IBM Storage Scale + Thales CipherTrust Manager 文件加密以及多层安全架构，在单一平台中实现数据全生命周期（静态、传输、使用中）加密，兼顾高性能与高安全性，并通过生物医学基准测试验证了性能损耗可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 HPC 系统追求算力速度，高度安全的系统则以牺牲性能为代价。然而生命科学等领域（如基因组学、医学影像）需要处理敏感数据，既要求合规（GDPR、ISO/IEC 27001、FIPS），又需要大规模 HPC 算力支持。现有方案存在局限：纯 ISO 认证中心缺乏技术防护，Trusted Research Environments (TRE) 主要依赖软件隔离无法保护"使用中的数据"，云端机密计算则需将数据外迁至第三方，不受研究人员控制。如何在共享多租户 HPC 环境中实现覆盖数据全生命周期的端到端安全与性能平衡，是开放性挑战。

Method: 1. **硬件层加密**：基于 AMD EPYC Genoa 处理器启用 SME/SEV/SEV-SNP，通过 KVM/QEMU 在固件层激活每 VM 独立内存加密密钥，提供数据"使用中"保护。<br>2. **文件加密**：采用 IBM Storage Scale (GPFS) + Thales CipherTrust Manager + Luna K7 HSM，实现三级密钥架构——FEK（每文件唯一）、MEK（每文件集，加密 FEK）、RKM（通过 KMIP 协议管理 MEK，密钥永不离开 HSM）。<br>3. **虚拟化与网络**：通过 SR-IOV 虚拟化 ConnectX-6 InfiniBand，单卡支持 127 个 VF 分配至不同 VM；VM 基于不可变磁盘镜像运行，运行后销毁，仅加密输出文件持久化。<br>4. **调度层集成**：自定义 Slurm SPANK 插件验证用户授权并自动启动对应安全 VM（仅需添加一个 Slurm 参数）。<br>5. **多层安全体系**：四层安全级（S0-S3），单向特权访问、严格防火墙策略；VPN + SSH 密钥 + Cisco Duo 强制 MFA；基于 ISO/IEC 27001 实施 ISMS。<br>6. **用户友好性**：Go + JavaScript 实现的 Web 端 SSH 密钥生成上传工具；公开 GitHub 文档与教程。

Result: 使用 RepeatMasker（I/O 密集）和 BWA-MEM2（内存密集）两个生命科学工作负载，在 7 种加密配置下（每种 6 次重复）进行基准测试：<br>- **完整安全栈相对裸金属 SMP 开销**：RepeatMasker 仅 +4.4%，BWA-MEM2 为 +18.0%。<br>- **开销分解**：约一半开销源自虚拟化层（KVM/QEMU），其余主要来自内存加密（在内存密集型工作负载中尤为明显）；文件加密在所有配置下性能影响可忽略不计。<br>- **CPU 效率**：RepeatMasker（并行-串行交替型）91%，BWA-MEM2 68%，加密配置下仍维持较高吞吐。<br>- **合规性**：满足 GDPR、ISO/IEC 27001、FIPS 等要求，并超出 ISO 27001 标准要求。<br>- **结论**：RAMSES 能在可接受性能范围内运行生产级敏感数据工作负载，开销与近期机密计算研究一致，但具有数据不出校园、可定制审计与策略控制的本地化优势。

Conclusion: RAMSES 证明了在多租户 HPC 系统中同时满足严格数据保护要求（GDPR、ISO/IEC 27001、NIST）与科研生产力是可行的。其核心贡献在于：<br>1. **超越现有安全 HPC**：相较仅做 ISO 认证或 TRE 模式的方案，RAMSES 通过 SEV 硬件加密将保护扩展至"使用中的数据"，填补了 NIST SP 800-223 提出的 gap。<br>2. **数据主权**：提供与云端机密计算（AMD SEV-SNP/Intel TDX）相同硬件级保证，但数据保留在大学信任边界内，支持详细审计与定制策略。<br>3. **可用性**：单个 Slurm 参数即可激活安全执行环境，无需修改用户工作流。<br>4. **未来方向**：VM 自动化与安全日志、轻量化容器隔离（Kata Containers）、NUMA 感知调优、向 AMD/NVIDIA GPU 扩展机密计算能力。<br>该系统为下一代敏感数据科学 HPC 平台提供了实用蓝图，并以开源 SPANK 插件/VM 配置代码（学术机构可索取）支持社区复用。

Abstract: Traditionally, the architecture of high-performance computing (HPC) systems is tailored for speed, while highly secure computer systems must sacrifice speed for security. However, a wide range of scientific domains, such as the life sciences, call for a combination of performance and security to allow processing sensitive data at scale. Here, we present RAMSES (Research Accelerator for Modeling and Simulation with Enhanced Security), an HPC system designed from the ground up to deliver high performance within a robust security framework. RAMSES integrates hardware-based memory encryption of AMD processors with state-of-the-art file encryption from IBM Storage Scale and the Thales CipherTrust manager, establishing an HPC platform that ensures continuous encryption throughout the data life cycle - at rest, in transit, and in use - in compliance with major data protection standards (European General Data Protection Regulation, ISO/IEC 27001 certification, and Federal Information Processing Standards). In addition, we implemented advanced operating system hardening, a multi-layered security architecture, and mandatory multi-factor authentication to adapt the HPC environment to increased security demands. Benchmark results from the biomedical sector demonstrate that the performance impact of the secure environment is limited and that integration of the conflicting requirements speed and security can be achieved while preserving a coherent, flexible, and user-friendly system.

</details>
