### [53] [Bifocal Diffusion Language Models: Asymmetric Bidirectional Context for Parallel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27732)
*Yuhang Chen,Xianfeng Wu,Jinhao Duan,Mingfu Liang,Xiaohan Wei,Yunchen Pu,Fei Tian,Chonglin Sun,Parish Aggarwal,Frank Shyu,Luke Simon,Sandeep Pandey,Xi Liu,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"双焦（Dual-Focus）"的新型离散扩散语言模型（Bifocal dLLM）范式，以解决传统dLLM中质量与效率不可兼得的核心架构难题。其具体实现为R2LM（Right-to-Left Mamba），即在保持因果注意力机制（兼容KV缓存）的基础上，引入一个轻量级的反向Mamba SSM旁路模块来提供压缩的右侧上下文信息。该方法在Qwen3-1.7B的60B token持续预训练实验中，相较双向dLLM实现了2.4×–12.9×的吞吐量提升，相较AR基线在批量服务中实现了1.9×–2.9×的加速，并在多数基准测试上超越了因果基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散语言模型（dLLM）通过并行恢复被掩码的token，相较自回归（AR）生成具有显著的吞吐量优势。然而该范式面临一个根本性的架构设计困境：①采用双向注意力虽能获得强生成质量（每个位置可访问完整上下文），但与KV缓存机制本质不兼容，限制了批处理服务场景下的推理吞吐；②采用因果注意力虽能实现高效缓存推理，但丧失了所有右侧上下文信息，导致生成质量显著下降。现有方案（块扩散、混合AR-扩散等）均未从根本上解决此矛盾：因果注意力必然限制每位置仅能获得左侧上下文，任何推理优化都无法恢复缺失的右侧信息。

Method: 核心方法是提出"非对称双向上下文（Asymmetric Bidirectional Context）"设计原则，并实例化为R2LM架构：（1）信息论基础：利用加性对数后验分解（Proposition 1）将位置i的最优对数后验分解为左侧上下文项与右侧上下文修正项 $\Delta_i^{\text{R2L}}$，证明其期望等于双向与因果之间的互信息缺口；（2）R2L流（Right-to-Left Stream）：包含四步操作——序列翻转（flip）→ Mamba选择性扫描（linear-time获取位置感知的右侧token摘要）→ 反向翻转与LayerNorm → tanh门控残差（$\mathbf{h}^+ = \mathbf{h} + \mathbf{h}_{\text{R2L}} \cdot \tanh(s)$，标量门s初始化为0）；（3）旁路挂载：通过前向钩子（forward hooks）将R2L流附加到预训练自回归骨干网络的每k层解码器中，因零初始化设计使增强模型在初始化时与因果Transformer位级一致；（4）训练模式：支持联合持续预训练和冻结骨干的即插即用模式（R2LM-PI），后者仅需5B额外token即可恢复联合模型59%的长目标增益；（5）推理优化：采用标准缓存并行解码，预填充阶段缓存prompt的KV及后钩子隐藏状态，去噪阶段仅处理G个生成位置，使R2L成本与P无关。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B模型上进行60B token的受控三方对比持续预训练实验，R2LM在单H100、P=4096、B=8的批量服务场景下取得以下结果：①吞吐量方面，R2LM相较双向dLLM实现2.4×–12.9×的提升，相较AR基线实现1.9×–2.9×的加速（通过缓存并行解码）；②质量方面，R2LM在多数基准测试上超越因果基线，在ALL平均值上超越双向dLLM；③即插即用模式（R2LM-PI）仅用5B额外token即恢复联合模型59%的长目标增益，验证了R2L流贡献的独立性和可分离性。R2LM定位于质量-吞吐量帕累托前沿的右上角区域（高质量+高吞吐），既非纯因果也非纯双向，而是独立的新范式。

Conclusion: Bifocal dLLM作为一种新范式，突破了dLLM中质量与效率之间长期存在的架构权衡难题。其核心贡献在于：①首次提出"非对称双向上下文"原则，通过非注意力通路（SSM旁路）提供连续的、位置感知的右侧上下文，同时保持标准因果前缀KV缓存的有效性；②实例化的R2LM架构以零初始化门控和位级一致初始化实现了对预训练自回归骨干的无扰动增强；③该范式将右侧信息通路的设计空间开放给更广泛的非注意力机制（如RNN、线性注意力等），为未来dLLM架构设计提供了新的方向。R2LM的成功证明：在不破坏因果注意力缓存性的前提下，右侧上下文信息可通过低成本SSM以压缩形式有效补偿，从而实现双向dLLM的生成质量与因果dLLM的推理效率的兼得。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models (dLLMs) recover masked tokens in parallel, offering significant speedups over autoregressive (AR) generation. However, such promising frameworks face a fundamental architectural design dilemma: \ding{182} Adopting bidirectional attention achieves strong generation quality by allowing each position to access the full context, but is inherently incompatible with KV caching, limiting inference throughput in batch-serving scenarios; \ding{183} Conversely, causal attention enables efficient cached inference but loses all right-side context, substantially degrading generation quality. This paper introduces Bifocal dLLMs, a new paradigm that resolves this dilemma through \emph{asymmetric bidirectional context}. Analogous to bifocal lenses, we instantiate the paradigm as \textbf{R2LM} (Right-to-Left Mamba), which combines two complementary mechanisms: $a$) standard causal attention providing precise left-context with full KV cache compatibility, while $b$) a lightweight reverse Mamba SSM sidecar supplying compressed right-side context without breaking cacheability. Comprehensive experiments on continued pretraining of Qwen3-1.7B with 60B tokens demonstrate that R2LM achieves $2.4\times$ to $12.9\times$ higher throughput than bidirectional dLLMs and $1.9\times$ to $2.9\times$ speedup over AR baselines in batch serving through parallel decoding with KV caching, while exceeding the causal baseline on most benchmarks and surpassing the bidirectional dLLM on average.

</details>


### [54] [End-to-End Dynamic Sparsity for Resource-Adaptive LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27743)
*Yuhang Chen,Jinhao Duan,Ruichen Zhang,Mingfu Liang,Xiaohan Wei,Yunchen Pu,Fei Tian,Chonglin Sun,Parish Aggarwal,Frank Shyu,Luke Simon,Sandeep Pandey,Tianlong Chen,Xi Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出L2A（Learning to Allocate）框架，通过预算条件门控网络使LLM推理能够根据运行时资源预算和输入难度动态调整计算量，沿层跳过、注意力头剪枝和推理令牌缩减三个维度实现端到端的资源自适应推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实云基础设施是动态的（spot实例抢占、QoS分级），而当前LLM推理采用静态计算图，缺乏弹性：在资源紧张时会崩溃或浪费算力。现有方法要么只依赖输入难度做早期退出，要么是基于静态压缩产生固定模型，无法应对实时资源波动和分层服务质量需求。

Method: 将推理建模为受输入和运行时预算b共同约束的资源分配问题。在冻结LLM中注入三类轻量级预算条件门控网络（MLP+sigmoid）：层跳过门α_l、注意力头门β_{l,h}、思维链转答案过渡门τ_t；通过LoRA（rank=16）进行参数高效适配。统一损失函数联合优化：交叉熵损失、KL蒸馏损失（防逻辑漂移）、计算量惩罚项（强制预算匹配）和思维链长度惩罚项。推理时门值硬阈值化实现真实加速，预算b由在线信号（SLA、spot剩余时间、内存余量、队列长度）校准到[0,1]区间。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B和Qwen-3-4B上的实验表明：单个L2A模型即可描绘完整计算-精度Pareto前沿，在34%层稀疏度下GSM8K仅比密集基线低0.6%，在HumanEval、BBH等分布外任务上同样保持小差距，而静态或启发式基线在相当推理时间下精度下降5-10%。消融实验显示：去除蒸馏损失γ导致GSM8K从35.8%崩溃至14.4%；去除预算损失λ则模型不退化；单独依赖层跳过需62.5%稀疏度才能达到同等延迟，性能显著劣于层+头联合策略。

Conclusion: L2A通过预算条件门控与统一优化目标，使单个LLM能在不同运行时资源预算下自适应调整计算足迹，在保持接近密集模型性能的同时显著降低推理成本，且对动态云环境和分层QoS场景具有更强的弹性与鲁棒性。局限性包括头部剪枝的加速效果依赖实现、预算校准需要可靠的在线延迟/内存估计、结构化<think>/answer格式对提示工程有依赖。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) inference is typically deployed under a static resource assumption, where models execute a fixed computational graph regardless of the runtime environment. However, real-world cloud infrastructure is inherently dynamic, characterized by fluctuating availability (e.g., spot instance preemption) and tiered Quality-of-Service requirements. In such volatile settings, static models are inflexible: they either crash under resource constraints or waste compute on redundant operations. To bridge this gap, we propose Learning to Allocate (L2A), an end-to-end framework for resource-adaptive inference. Unlike prior methods that condition only on input difficulty, we formulate inference as a constrained allocation problem conditioned on both the input and the runtime resource budget itself. We introduce lightweight, budget-conditioned and input-aware gating networks integrated into the LLM. These gates are trained via a unified objective that jointly optimizes task performance, logical consistency, and resource costs along three axes matching how real-world dynamics manifest: layer skipping for memory and depth pressure, head pruning for throughput contention, and reasoning-token reduction for latency tightening. This lets the model learn a budget-aware policy beyond input difficulty alone: it adaptively configures its computational footprint with respect to real-time resource dynamics, maximizing reasoning depth when resources permit while enforcing strict frugality when budgets tighten. A single L2A model traces the entire compute-accuracy Pareto frontier on Llama-3-8B and Qwen-3-4B: at up to 34% realized layer sparsity, it stays within 0.6% of the dense baseline on GSM8K, with the same gap holding zero-shot on out-of-distribution tasks, while every static or heuristic baseline requires a separately tuned model and still drops by 5-10% at comparable inference time.

</details>


### [55] [A Sensitivity-Aware Test Collection for Search Among Personal Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27559)
*Jack McKechnie,Graham McDonald,Craig Macdonald*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了SARA（Sensitivity-Aware Relevance Assessments）测试集合，基于公开免费的Enron邮件语料库构建，包含129,821篇文档、50个主题的150个查询、11,471条人工相关性评估以及LLM自动生成的相关性和敏感性标签，为敏感信息感知搜索（SAS）系统的开发提供了可免费获取、无许可限制的基准资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 敏感信息感知搜索（SAS）旨在从包含敏感个人信息的文档集合中检索出相关但不敏感的结果。然而现有测试集合存在诸多局限：TREC 2010 Legal Track中文档的相关性和敏感性评估重叠度小；Avocado集合虽然有用但需约1,500美元且许可严格，限制了用户研究和众包实验的开展。因此，研究社区迫切需要一个免费、开放、包含查询、相关性评估和敏感性标签的测试集合来推动SAS研究。

Method: 1）文档预处理：使用CMU版Enron语料（约50万文档），通过MD5哈希去重，使用QualT5去除0.1%低质量文档，并预留10万文档作为训练集，最终保留129,821篇文档；2）信息需求构建：使用LDA主题建模识别50个主题，人工核验后撰写信息需求描述；3）查询收集：通过Prolific平台众包收集150个查询（每个主题10个），采用£7/小时薪酬和注意力检测；4）相关性评估：第一阶段在Hearst子集上通过18个系统构建top-20池，由众包工人对查询-文档对进行三级标注（高度相关/部分相关/不相关），共获得11,471条人工评估；第二阶段使用Llama 7B结合UMBRELA提示和少样本示例对全集合进行LLM相关性评估，并避免Dietz等人提出的四种LLM评估陷阱；5）敏感性标签：训练T5模型对Hearst子集进行分类（"纯个人/商业语境中的个人"为敏感），并应用于全部129,821篇文档。

Result: 1）相关性基线：LLM重排序器RankZephyr-7B表现最佳（nDCG@10=0.7429），交叉编码器次之，稀疏模型优于密集模型，密集模型优于词汇模型；2）敏感性分类基线：T5分类器最优（BAC=0.8428），优于LR和SVM，LLM中Qwen 72B优于Llama 8B，few-shot通常优于zero-shot；3）SAS基线：使用T5过滤器进行后过滤能在保持相关性的同时显著降低敏感文档比例（如BM25+monoT5+T5 Filter的nCSDCG@10达0.9688）；4）LLM评估有效性验证：人类与LLM相关性评估对56个检索系统的排序相关性高（Spearman ρ=0.8235，Kendall τ=0.6154），证明LLM评估可靠。

Conclusion: SARA集合是Enron邮件语料库的扩展，包含查询、人工与LLM相关性评估及敏感性标签，免费通过ir_datasets包和Huggingface提供，并附带稀疏和密集索引支持便捷实验。该集合填补了SAS研究领域缺乏免费开放测试集合的空白，可与Avocado集合配合使用以验证方法的可泛化性，实验表明其在相关性、敏感性分类和SAS三个任务上都能呈现合理的基线性能趋势，为未来敏感信息感知检索模型的开发提供了重要资源。

Abstract: Traditional search tasks aim to satisfy user information needs by returning a subset of a collection of documents, ranked by the documents' relevance to a user query. However, some collections that contain useful information also contain sensitive personal information. Recently, there has been increasing interest in the development of Sensitivity-Aware Search (SAS) retrieval models to provide users with effective retrieval results without revealing such sensitive information. To develop such systems, test collections containing both sensitive and non-sensitive information, a set of queries, and query-document relevance assessments are required. The Enron email corpus contains real business-related emails, where some emails also contain sensitive personal information. However, the original Enron collection does not contain queries or query-relevance assessments. To this end, we crowdsource 150 query formulations for 50 different topics and 11,471 query-relevance assessments for a subset of the Enron documents that have been manually labelled for sensitivity. We follow best practices for using large language models (LLMs) in Information Retrieval evaluation to extend the collection further with additional LLM judged query-relevance assessments and sensitivity labels. We present baseline performances for relevance, sensitivity classification, and sensitivity-aware search on the collection. We make the collection available, including through the popular ir_datasets package, and provide pre-built sparse and dense indices on Huggingface to facilitate easy experimentation.

</details>


### [56] [Intuition-Guided Latent Reasoning for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27684)
*Chang Liu,Yimeng Bai,Xiaoyan Zhao,Yang Zhang,Qifan Wang,Fuli Feng,Wenge Rong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: IntuRec是一个两阶段框架，通过从LLM推荐器中提取top-K候选集作为"推荐直觉"(recommendation intuition)的来源，并利用自注意力和交叉注意力机制将其编码为偏好对齐的直觉嵌入，作为潜在推理的起点，从而解决现有潜在推理方法推理起点与目标物品嵌入不对齐的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐系统中的潜在推理方法从无约束的推理起点开始，导致隐藏表示与目标物品嵌入不对齐，可能使推理轨迹偏向不相关或次优区域。受认知神经科学启发（人类多步推理由直觉作为潜在先验引导），作者提出在潜在推理过程中锚定"推荐直觉"，以提供语义有依据的起点。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1)直觉源提取(ISE)：训练LLM推荐器进行序列推荐，使用束搜索生成top-K正向候选集c+和随机负向候选集c-，提出目标感知候选平衡策略(TACB)调节训练/验证中目标物品的包含比例以避免捷径学习；(2)直觉表示注入(IRI)：通过直觉双注意力编码器(IDAE)对候选集编码——先对每个候选物品进行自注意力+末位池化得到物品级表示，再用交叉注意力将初始推理状态r1作为query与物品表示融合，得到直觉嵌入r1+；采用BPR对比损失监督直觉对齐；总体损失为下一物品预测损失与直觉对齐损失的加权和。

Result: 在Amazon CDs、Toys、Games三个数据集上的实验表明，IntuRec一致优于BIGRec、D³、LatentR³等基线方法；以D³为骨干的IntuRec-D达到最优性能。消融实验验证了TACB、IDAE、自/交叉注意力和BPR损失的必要性。在Instruments和Books数据集上的额外验证也证实了方法的有效性。可视化分析显示直觉嵌入相比无约束的潜在推理起点显著更接近目标物品。可设为K=15、N=1时性能最佳。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将认知神经科学中的"直觉"概念引入LLM推荐系统的潜在推理，提出IntuRec框架通过提取和注入偏好对齐的直觉嵌入来初始化推理过程，有效提升了推理轨迹的准确性和效率。直觉嵌入相当于一个"指南针"，引导推理沿偏好子空间进行。推理阶段比LatentR³更快，因为直觉聚合仅在第一步执行。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive reasoning capabilities in complex problem-solving tasks, motivating their use for preference reasoning in recommender systems. Latent reasoning, which operates in continuous hidden spaces rather than discrete tokens, has recently emerged as a promising paradigm for LLM-based recommendation. However, existing methods often start from unconstrained reasoning points, where hidden representations are misaligned with target item embeddings, leading to suboptimal reasoning trajectories. Inspired by cognitive neuroscience, which suggests that human multi-step reasoning is guided by intuition as a latent prior, we propose \emph{IntuRec}, a two-stage framework that anchors latent reasoning with \emph{recommendation intuition}. In the extraction stage, the LLM-based recommender generates a top-$K$ candidate set based on users' histories as the source of intuition. In the injection stage, the candidate set is transformed into a preference-aligned intuition embedding using self- and cross-attention mechanisms, which initializes the reasoning start point and guides subsequent latent reasoning. By providing a semantically grounded starting point, IntuRec efficiently explores the preference space along more accurate reasoning trajectories. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that IntuRec consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. We release our code at this https URL .

</details>


### [57] [From Bootstrapping to Sequence Modeling: A Unified Generative Framework for Personalized Landing-Page Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27865)
*Fan Li,Chang Meng,Jiaqi Fu,Shuchang Liu,Tianke Zhang,Xueliang Wang,Xiaoqiang Feng,Yongqi Liu,Kaiqiao Zhan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出GLAN（Generative Landing-page Adaptive Navigator），一种基于Decision Transformer的序列建模框架，用于解决快手平台个性化落地页建模（PLPM）任务。针对现有CQL方法KLAN的两个根本缺陷（Markov假设无法捕捉非马尔可夫时序依赖、TD bootstrapping在长时域延迟奖励下存在累积误差和信用分配难题），GLAN引入两个关键模块：L-RTG（拉格朗日约束Return-to-Go）模块捕捉用户跨日消费动态以提供全局指导，HRM（层次化奖励模型）模块将会话级反馈分解为细粒度信号以提供精准局部监督。在快手平台线上A/B测试中（5%流量，约56天），GLAN在DAU和LT上分别取得+0.158%和+0.108%的显著提升，已全量部署服务数亿日活用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化落地页建模（PLPM）是在多页架构在线平台中为用户进入应用时选择最佳功能页的关键决策问题。快手此前提出的KLAN框架基于保守Q学习（CQL），存在两个根本性局限：（1）马尔可夫假设无法捕捉真实用户行为中强烈的非马尔可夫时序依赖；（2）时序差分学习的bootstrapping机制在延迟奖励、长时域场景下产生严重的累积误差和信用分配困难（用户每天多次进入应用）。此外，将Decision Transformer应用于工业级PLPM还面临两个关键挑战：跨日RTG建模的初始化困境（过激或过保守的RTG目标分别导致OOD状态和模型幻觉或无法挖掘高价值用户潜力），以及会话级奖励的信用模糊性（相同会话总时长可能对应截然不同的落地页质量）。

Method: GLAN采用"全局-局部"统一视角的序列建模框架，包含三大核心组件：

1. **L-RTG模块（全局指导）**：预测用户当日预期总消费时长作为初始Return-to-Go。包含三部分：(a) **周期性感知特征表示**——通过引入基于模运算的周期性偏置（mod 7的星期偏置 + 时间距离桶偏置）的注意力机制捕捉用户稳定的周级周期行为；(b) **序列动态建模**——使用标准Transformer编码器+注意力池化捕捉兴趣演变的随机波动；(c) **自适应门控机制**——多专家MMoE结构，针对App使用时长和会话频次两个任务分别通过任务特定门控网络聚合周期性与动态表示。训练时将L-RTG建模形式化为约束优化问题：主目标为App使用时长预测精度（Huber Loss），约束为会话频次MSE损失以及两者结构一致性损失（利用在线数据中两者近似对数线性关系），通过Lagrangian对偶优化求解。

2. **HRM模块（局部监督）**：对会话级价值进行精准评估。(a) **特征选择与融合**——使用目标注意力机制，以落地页嵌入为查询对用户/上下文/会话特征进行页面特定的软选择；(b) **多任务预测**——采用MMoE共享骨干+四个独立塔，预测三类页面的消费时长和落地页跳出风险（sigmoid二分类）；(c) 训练损失为细粒度Huber回归损失 + Focal BCE跳出风险惩罚（γ聚焦难样本），缓解类不平衡和过度自信问题。推理时奖励计算为：r̂ = (1 - ŷ_sw) · ΣT̂^j，对高跳出风险会话进行折扣。

3. **Decision Transformer主干**：将PLPM形式化为序列建模任务，轨迹表示为(R̂₁,s₁,a₁,...,R̂_t,s_t,a_t)，利用因果自注意力捕捉长程时序依赖。在线推理时形成闭环：每日开始调用L-RTG初始化R̂₁；每次进入应用时DT以当前状态、剩余RTG和历史轨迹为条件自回归生成落地页动作；会话结束后由HRM评估奖励r̂，并按R̂_{t+1} = R̂_t - r̂更新RTG。

Result: 在快手平台约5%流量、56天（14天AA + 42天AB）线上A/B测试中，GLAN相对在线基线KLAN取得全面显著提升（p<0.05）：DAU +0.158%、LT +0.108%、App使用时长 +0.369%、观看时长 +0.394%、视频播放量 +0.469%、落地页跳出率 -15.832%、整体有效进页频次 +1.079%、延迟仅+0.087%。消融实验显示：去除L-RTG（用过去7天均值初始化）下降最大（App时长+0.137%、观看时长+0.175%、视频量+0.158%），表明静态启发式无法捕捉跨日波动；去除HRM（用总会话时长作为奖励）也带来明显退化（+0.277%/+0.278%/+0.404%），证明粗粒度会话奖励无法精准信用分配。两者结合产生协同增益。深度分析表明，GLAN有效打破了Featured Page的"赢者通吃"现象：Featured分配比-2.481%，Explore +5.264%，Following +8.461%，且三页有效进页频次均正增长，证明流量重分配真实匹配了用户偏好。目前GLAN已在快手全量部署。

Conclusion: 本文系统分析了现有CQL-based KLAN框架在PLPM任务上的根本局限，验证了序列建模范式相比传统RL的优势。提出的GLAN框架通过L-RTG（解决非马尔可夫时序依赖和跨日RTG初始化困境）与HRM（解决会话级信用分配模糊）的全局-局部统一建模，有效提升了落地页分配质量。广泛的在线实验证明其有效性和可扩展性，GLAN已成功在快手平台全量部署，服务数亿日活用户。未来工作方向未在文中明确展开，但研究框架对工业级长时域序列决策问题具有方法论参考价值。

Abstract: Modern online platforms increasingly adopt multi-page architectures to accommodate diverse user needs. On these platforms, page navigation (the process of directing users to specific functional pages upon app entry) serves as a critical gateway that shapes user's first impression and significantly influences subsequent engagement. To optimize this process, Kuaishou formulated the task of Personalized Landing Page Modeling (PLPM) and proposed KLAN, a reinforcement learning framework built upon Conservative Q-Learning (CQL). However, CQL-based approaches suffer from two fundamental limitations: (1) the Markov assumption fails to capture the strong non-Markovian temporal dependencies inherent in real-world user behaviors, and (2) TD learning with bootstrapping incurs severe cumulative errors and credit assignment difficulties under delayed rewards, particularly in long-horizon settings where users enter the app multiple times daily. To address these limitations, we propose GLAN (Generative Landing-page Adaptive Navigator), a sequence modeling framework built on Decision Transformer to tackle PLPM from a unified global-local perspective. Specifically, GLAN incorporates two key modules. First, we design the L-RTG module that captures users' inter-day consumption dynamics to provide accurate global guidance for all page assignments within a day. Furthermore, we propose the HRM module that decomposes session-level feedback into fine-grained signals, enabling precise local supervision for each page assignment. Extensive online experiments conducted on the Kuaishou platform demonstrate the effectiveness of GLAN, achieving +0.158\% and +0.108\% improvements on Daily Active Users (DAU) and user Lifetime (LT) respectively.

</details>


### [58] [An LLM-Powered Semantic Alignment Framework for Journal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27930)
*Yanglin Yan,Zicheng Xie,Tianchen Gao,Rui Pan,Hansheng Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的语义对齐框架用于期刊推荐，将期刊推荐定义为稿件内容与期刊范围描述之间的语义匹配问题，无需任务特定训练即可直接推断期刊适配性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 期刊选择对研究者日益困难，尤其是对跨学科或处于职业早期的学者。现有期刊推荐系统多依赖监督学习、人工特征工程或历史交互数据，存在泛化性和可解释性不足的问题。大语言模型的兴起为以自然语言方式联合评估稿件与期刊提供了新可能，但其在学术期刊推荐场景中的应用尚未充分探索。

Method: 1. 构建数据集：基于 Web of Science 收集 23,609 篇来自 49 个统计及相关领域期刊（2021-2025）的文章，以及 2,683 篇来自 8 个核心统计期刊的引用增强数据集（含参考文献信息）。2. 收集每篇候选期刊的官方 Aims & Scope 声明。3. 设计统一提示框架，将期刊推荐建模为自然语言结构化决策任务，将文章标题、摘要、关键词（及参考文献信息）与候选期刊范围描述输入 LLM（DeepSeek-V3）进行语义匹配排序。4. 对比有/无引用信息、不同 Top-K 截断下的推荐效果，并进行稳定性与推理行为分析。

Result: 主要实验结果：Top-3、Top-5、Top-10 准确率分别达到 40.23%、53.67% 和 70.05%。额外分析显示：（1）引入参考文献信息在多数情况下提升推荐性能；（2）推荐结果在多次重复运行中高度稳定，平均 Top-5 Jaccard 相似度达 84%；（3）框架能生成可解释的推理输出，准确推荐往往伴随清晰、信息丰富的推理过程。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了 LLM 作为免训练、可扩展范式在期刊推荐与学术决策支持中的潜力。语义对齐框架无需任务特定训练即可有效工作，引用信息具有增益效果，且模型推理可解释性强。这些发现为 AI 辅助期刊推荐系统的设计提供了实用参考，代码已在 GitHub 开源。

Abstract: Journal recommendation is an important task in scholarly information systems. Existing approaches typically rely on supervised learning models, manually engineered features, or historical interaction data, which may limit their generalizability and interpretability. We propose an LLM-powered semantic alignment framework that formulates journal recommendation as a semantic matching problem between manuscript content and journal scope descriptions. The framework enables large language models (LLMs) to infer journal suitability directly from article titles, abstracts, keywords, and candidate journal information without task-specific training. Experiments are conducted using DeepSeek-V3 on a dataset of 23,609 articles from 49 journals in statistics and related fields. The proposed framework achieves Top-3, Top-5, and Top-10 accuracies of 40.23\%, 53.67\%, and 70.05\%, respectively. Additional analyses show that incorporating reference information generally improves recommendation performance and that recommendations remain highly stable across repeated runs, with an average Top-5 Jaccard similarity of 84\%. The framework also generates interpretable reasoning outputs that provide insights into the recommendation process. These findings demonstrate the potential of LLMs as a training-free and scalable paradigm for journal recommendation and scholarly decision support.

</details>


### [59] [Listwise Explanation of Embedding-Based Rankings via Semantic Chunk Grouping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27980)
*Hyunkyu Kim,Yeeun Yoo,Youngjun Kwak*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ChunkGroupSHAP 是一种面向密集嵌入排序器的列表式 Shapley 解释方法。它通过将文档分块、嵌入并聚类为共享的跨文档语义特征组，解决了词级特征与密集语义表示之间的粒度不匹配问题。实验表明，最佳解释单元取决于具体场景：BM25 适合词级特征，密集排序器在领域语料上适合语料级 chunk 组，异构网络检索则适合查询局部分组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有列表式排序解释方法（如 RankSHAP、RankingSHAP）主要使用词级（词干化单词）作为解释特征单元。但密集嵌入排序器是基于句段级上下文语义表示打分的，词级特征过于碎片化，无法匹配密集排序器的表示粒度。同时，原始 chunk 虽语义合适但是文档特有的，破坏了列表式扰动机制（同一 mask 需跨文档共享特征）。这一"特征单元不匹配"问题亟待解决。

Method: ChunkGroupSHAP 方法分四步：1) 使用滑动窗口将文档分块；2) 用嵌入模型对 chunk 编码；3) 对 chunk 嵌入做 k-means 聚类形成 k 个共享语义组；4) 用 KernelSHAP 估计每个语义组的 Shapley 归因值。提供两种分组范围：语料级（corpus-level，跨查询可复用）和查询局部（query-local，适应当前候选列表）。使用 NDCG@K 作为列表级效用函数，扰动时仅保留与活跃组对应的 chunk 并重新打分。

Result: 在 MS MARCO、FinanceBench、AILACaseDocs、FinQA 四个数据集上，使用 E5 系列密集排序器和 BM25 评估：1) 密集排序器在领域/长文档语料（FinanceBench、AILACaseDocs、FinQA）上，ChunkGroupSHAP（语料级）保真度优于词级基线；2) BM25 在多数数据集上词级特征仍最强；3) MS MARCO（异构网络检索）上语料级分组表现欠佳，但查询局部分组大幅提升效果；4) 人类重排研究（7 名参与者，30 个查询）显示 ChunkGroupSHAP 查询局部版 NDCG@5=0.347，显著优于 RankSHAP 的 0.189；5) 随机分组的诊断表明高保真度并不必然意味着语义一致性。

Conclusion: 排序解释的特征单元应根据排序器的表示粒度和检索语料的结构共同决定，不应预先固定。词级特征适合词法排序器；语料级语义 chunk 组适合密集排序器在领域语料上的解释；查询局部分组则适合异构网络检索。这一原则对实际搜索与 RAG 系统的可解释性、调试与审计具有指导意义。

Abstract: Dense embedding rankers score documents through contextual sentence- and passage-level representations. Yet many listwise explanation methods still attribute rankings to isolated words. This feature-unit mismatch leaves word-level features too fragmented for dense semantic ranking. We introduce ChunkGroupSHAP, a listwise Shapley method that clusters semantically related chunks into shared cross-document features. Masking a group perturbs all documents with related evidence, attributing rankings at a granularity closer to dense representations while preserving the listwise setup. Our findings across MS MARCO, FinanceBench, AILACaseDocs, and FinQA with E5 rankers and BM25 show that the best explanation unit is setting-dependent: word features for lexical BM25, corpus-level groups for dense rankers, and query-local grouping for heterogeneous web retrieval. Feature units should thus follow both the ranker's representational granularity and the structure of the retrieved corpus.

</details>


### [60] [Fast and Feasible: Permutation-based Constrained Reranking for Revenue Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28059)
*Svetlana Shirokovskikh,Anastasiia Soboleva,Ekaterina Solodneva,Aleksandr Katrutsa,Roman Loginov,Egor Samosvat*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的排列式重排序近似算法PermR，用于电商搜索结果的重排序以最大化平台收入，同时通过约束保障相关性等指标不退化。该算法在大型分类信息平台Avito上进行了离线与在线实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在电商搜索系统中，仅追求收入最大化会损害相关性和用户体验。现有ILP求解器虽然能精确求解约束优化问题，但推理延迟过高无法在线部署；遗传算法在严格时间限制下收入提升有限。因此需要一种低延迟、可处理多约束的重排序近似算法。

Method: 1) 将收入感知重排序问题形式化为整数线性规划（ILP），目标为最大化收入指标F0，约束为相关性、反欺诈等多个指标不低于当前生产排名的基线值，并支持top-K前缀约束；2) 提出PermR算法：在每次迭代中检查所有约束是否满足，若满足则以概率正比于目标改善量采样相邻物品对进行交换以提升收入，若违反则随机选择违反的约束并以概率正比于该约束改善量采样交换以修复约束；当约束涉及前缀且无法通过交换改善时，将最高分项插入到合适前缀位置。

Result: 离线实验（N=50）：ILP求解器（MOSEK 12线程）平均耗时0.612秒，超出生产0.05秒的限制，理论收入提升+6.9%；PermR在750次迭代下耗时0.038秒，收入提升+4.3%，达到ILP收入提升的63%；遗传算法（0.05秒）仅+0.4%。在线A/B测试（14天，5600万查询，35%流量）：PermR平均收入提升+2.0%（全品类），其中Home & Garden提升最大（+6.0%），Fashion最稳定（+4.2%±0.8%），且所有平台约束均得到满足。

Conclusion: PermR在推理时间和收入提升之间提供了合理权衡，适合部署到生产环境。它无需训练排序模型、同时支持多约束、在生产延迟限制内近似达到ILP最优收入的63%，并通过在线A/B测试验证了在5600万查询上提升收入2%且不违反任何约束。

Abstract: Search and recommender systems have produced highly relevant search results. A natural next step in the development of such systems in e-commerce is to rerank these results to increase the platform's revenue from paid promotion products. However, maximizing revenue alone may degrade the user experience by reducing relevance or increasing fraud risk. To avoid this, we state the reranking problem as an integer linear program ($ILP$) that maximizes revenue subject to per-query constraints on other metrics, e.g., relevance. Since solving $ILP$ exactly for every query is slow for deployment to the online service, we propose a lightweight permutation-based reranking approximation algorithm PermR. At each step, the algorithm selects a pair of neighboring items and swaps them to either improve the objective or repair a violated constraint. We evaluate PermR across multiple categories of a large classified platform in offline and online settings. PermR achieves about 63\% of the ILP revenue improvement, within production latency limits, preserving all constraints. In a 14-day online A/B test over 56 million search queries, PermR increased revenue by $2$\%.

</details>

### [61] [AI-Model Network: Concept, Current State and Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27382)
*Li Zhetao,Zeng Xiyu,Wang Jianhui,Xiao Yong,Liu Zhongren,Wu Junru,Lai Junjie,Huang Jijun,Long Saiqin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"全球AI模型网络"（AI-ModelNet）概念，借鉴互联网发展经验，构建一个实现异构AI模型互联互通、能力共享与协同推理的新型范式，并通过原型系统与应用案例验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型（LMs）训练成本高、部署复杂，正向轻量化、私有化、领域化方向发展。然而，异构模型广泛分布却缺乏有效的交互与协作机制，已成为大模型发展的关键瓶颈。论文受互联网"互联共享"理念启发，尝试解决模型间的协同问题。

Method: 首先综述单模型与多模型研究现状；其次阐述AI-ModelNet的系统愿景与分层架构设计；最后构建原型系统并通过多样化应用案例对框架可行性进行验证。

Result: 论文提出了一套AI-ModelNet的分层系统架构设计方案，并通过原型系统与多个应用场景的实践，初步验证了该框架在实现模型互联、能力共享与协同推理方面的可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: AI-ModelNet为突破异构大模型协作瓶颈提供了新范式；未来需在关键研究方向上进一步深入探索，以推动该网络架构的落地与生态构建。

Abstract: While the primary function of computers lies in computation and processing, the core value of the Internet is rooted in sharing and collaboration. Computers create the Internet, and the Internet empowers the value of computers. The rapid development of the Internet, cloud computing, and big data is pushing artificial intelligence into the era of large models (LMs). However, the practical application of LMs is currently hindered by high training costs and deployment complexities, driving a shift toward lightweight, private, and domain-specific models. With the rapid proliferation and wide distribution of heterogeneous models, enabling effective interaction and collaboration among them has emerged as a critical bottleneck that urgently needs to be addressed in LM development. Drawing inspiration from the development of the Internet, this paper proposes the concept, vision, and system architecture of world wide AI-model network (AI-ModelNet). It is a novel paradigm that achieves interconnection, capability sharing, and collaborative reasoning by establishing pathways between models. We first briefly review the current state of single-model and multi-model research. Subsequently, the systemic vision and hierarchical architecture of AI-ModelNet are articulated, followed by validation of the framework's feasibility through a prototype system and diverse application cases. Finally, key directions for future research are discussed preliminarily.

</details>


### [62] [When Does Personality Composition Matter for Multi-Agent LLM Teams?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27443)
*Aryan Keluskar,Amrita Bhattacharjee,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多智能体LLM团队中人格组成对任务表现的影响，发现低宜人性（low agreeableness）提示在所有领域均产生显著沟通偏移（φ: 0.44→0.93），但其对客观任务结果的影响高度依赖任务结构——在结构化编码任务中几乎无影响（3/4模型），在开放式研究协作和竞争性议价任务中则严重降低表现。核心发现是"人工制品介导的缓冲"机制：结构化输出通过形式约束过滤过程退化，非结构化输出则直接暴露于沟通质量下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统（如Claude Code、OpenAI Codex、MetaGPT、AutoGen等）已从研究原型走向大规模产品部署，但团队的人格组成是否影响任务表现尚不明确。已有研究证明人格提示能塑造智能体沟通风格（高宜人性产生合作语言，低宜人性产生对抗语言），但这些沟通变化是否级联到客观任务结果、能否跨任务领域泛化，以及是否被负面形容词加载所夸大，均未被系统研究。这是从组织心理学延伸到LLM多智能体系统的核心问题，涉及到person-environment fit理论在人工系统中的适用性。

Method: 采用Goldberg双极形容词标记法对Big Five人格进行操控，通过7组双极形容词对和9级语言强度限定符（主要使用level 2/8对应低/高宜人性）构建人格提示。任务设计基于"人工制品结构"和"目标对齐"两维分类，选择三个任务域：（1）编码任务（MultiAgentBench中3智能体协作软件开发，合作型+高结构）；（2）研究任务（5智能体开放式研究想法生成，合作型+低结构）；（3）议价任务（2智能体买卖谈判，竞争型+低结构）。评测模型包括Claude Sonnet 4.5、GPT-4o、Grok-3和DeepSeek V3.1。沟通测量采用Bales交互过程分析框架的改编版，将消息分段后用GPT-4o-mini标注四类沟通行为（问题、异议、建议、认同），计算沟通状态φ=探索性行为/（探索性+认同性）。研究包括三个RQ：人格是否影响客观结果（RQ1）、效应是否跨域泛化（RQ2）、效应是否被负面形容词夸大（RQ3），并辅以中性改述实验和异质团队试点。

Result: RQ1结果：低宜人性在所有模型和领域均产生一致的大幅沟通偏移（φ: 0.44→0.93），但仅在宜人性维度出现此效应，尽责性和开放性的消融实验产生最小沟通变化。RQ2结果：效应具有任务条件性——编码任务中里程碑完成率几乎不变（3/4模型，Claude d=0.06、GPT-4o d=0.51 n.s.、DeepSeek d=0.43 n.s.，仅Grok-3显著下降d=1.69）；研究任务中所有3个测试模型均显著下降（GPT-4o 10.5→3.5，d=1.41；Grok-3 17.0→11.8，d=1.35；DeepSeek 9.7→5.8，d=0.79）；议价任务中协议率崩溃至≤1%（高宜人性则约翻倍）。LLM评判的代码质量也无显著退化。RQ3结果：Goldberg低宜人性提示使所有模型收敛到φ≈0.93（跨度0.02），而中性改述使模型发散（跨度0.45）；中性改述虽减弱但不消除效应，表明效应被负面形容词放大但非纯粹由其引起。机制分解比δ=c_D/(c_Q+c_D+c_S)揭示两条路径：异议主导型（Claude/GPT-4o/Grok-3，δ≈0.48-0.55）和建议主导型（DeepSeek，δ≈0.11）。异质团队试点表明：领头位置的挑战者比全员低宜人性团队危害小得多，甚至可匹配基线。

Conclusion: 人格提示对多智能体LLM团队沟通的影响客观存在，但结果效应取决于任务输出介质是否提供结构约束来缓冲过程退化。结构化输出（代码文件）通过形式属性（语法、类型系统、功能规范）约束收敛，使结果免受沟通退化的影响；非结构化输出（研究想法、谈判报价）缺乏此类约束，结果直接暴露于沟通质量。跨模型在标准提示下收敛到对抗性特征在很大程度上被加载形容词触发的安全相关响应所放大；在非毒性提示下，真正的特征效应具有模型特异性、衰减性且方向一致。RLHF和宪法AI训练使前沿LLM高度合作，宜人性维度饱和，因此人格提示主要是一种诱导对抗行为的技术而非增强合作。设计建议：低宜人性在异质团队中应作为有界的、领头位置的批评者角色部署，而非团队范围的特征。该研究为构建可预测、有效的智能体团队提供了人格-任务结构交互的关键见解。

Abstract: Personality prompting shapes how large language models communicate, yet whether these behavioral shifts affect objective task outcomes remains under-explored. Prior work shows that agents prompted with low agreeableness produce adversarial language, while those prompted with high agreeableness become cooperative, but the relationship between communication style and task performance has not been systematically examined across multiple domains. In this work, we investigate whether personality composition matters for multi-agent team performance by manipulating personality traits across frontier LLMs on three task domains: structured coding, open-ended research collaboration, and competitive bargaining. We find that personality effects depend critically on task structure. In coding tasks, low agreeableness leads to large communication shifts that have little effect on milestone completion. In open-ended collaboration and bargaining, the same manipulation substantially degrades performance. We discuss implications for multi-agent system design and the limits of personality manipulation.

</details>


### [63] [Internalizing the Future: A Unified Agentic Training Paradigm for World Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27483)
*Xuan Zhang,Zhijian Zhou,Lingfeng Qiao,Yulei Qin,Ke Li,Xing Sun,Xiaoyu Tan,Chao Qu,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三阶段训练范式,通过在LLM智能体中内部化"世界模型"来实现未来感知规划,使单个自回归模型能够同时生成前瞻性状态推演和计划条件下的成功估计(类似Q值的文本类比),从而克服标准LLM智能体在长视野任务中仅具有反应性而缺乏"假设性"推理能力的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LLM智能体在顺序决策任务中表现强大,但本质上仍是被动反应式的(reactive),缺乏类似人类的"what-if"反事实推理能力——即在承诺执行计划前,无法通过内部世界模型模拟未来结果。简单的后训练(如在look-ahead轨迹上微调)只能导致表面的模仿(foresight的形似),而非真正具有预测基础(predictive grounding)的远见,即存在"格式-能力差距"(format-capability gap)。

Method: 提出三阶段训练流程:(1) **WM-AMT (World Model Agentic Mid-Training)**:在世界模型轨迹上进行智能体式中期训练,将潜在的预测能力注入到策略模型中;(2) **FE-SFT (Format-Eliciting SFT)**:通过监督微调将已注入的预测能力结构化为期望的输出格式(前瞻状态+成功估计);(3) **FC-RL (Foresight-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning)**:通过强化学习进一步精炼生成模拟的校准性和实用性。整体架构为单一自回归模型,联合产生state rollout与plan-conditioned textual Q-value。

Result: 在搜索(search)和数学推理任务上的评估显示,该方法一致优于其他训练基线,证明"能力优先"(capability-first)训练流水线能够使LLM智能体获得有基础且经过校准的内部世界建模与远见能力。

Conclusion: LLM智能体要实现有效的内部世界建模,关键不在于简单地让模型模仿前瞻性输出格式,而在于先通过中期训练注入真正的预测能力,再结构化其输出,最后通过强化学习校准其效用。即"capability-first"训练范式是实现grounded and calibrated foresight的必要路径。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong capability in sequential decision-making, yet they remains fundamentally reactive in long-horizon tasks. Unlike humans who employ "what-if" reasoning to evaluate potential plans before commitment, standard agents lack an internal world model to simulate future outcomes. Therefore, we propose to internalize future-aware planning by training a single autoregressive model to verbalize both a prospective state rollout and a plan-conditioned success estimate-a textual analogue of the Q-value. Crucially, we identify a format-capability gap: simply fine-tuning agents on look-ahead traces during post-training leads to superficial mimicry of foresight without genuine predictive grounding. To bridge this gap, we introduce a three-stage training paradigm: (i) World Model Agentic Mid-Training (WM-AMT) to inject latent predictive capabilities into the policy; (ii) Format-Eliciting SFT (FE-SFT) to structure this injected capability; and (iii) Foresight-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (FC-RL) to refine the calibration and utility of the generated simulations. Evaluated on search and mathematical reasoning tasks, our approach consistently outperforms other training baselines. Our results demonstrate that effective internal world modeling in LLM agents requires a capability-first training pipeline to achieve grounded and calibrated foresight.

</details>


### [64] [Odyssey: Constructing Verifiable Local Truth-Preserving Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27593)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ODYSSEY框架，将基础模型构建重新定义为可检查"铸造厂"(foundry)的设计，即局部预测、逻辑和证据模型的层状族(类层)。系统采用五代理架构(Scylla/Homer/Athena/Prometheus/Toulmin)，通过范畴论工具(左/右Kan扩张)和有限真值集合实现局部性、可追溯性和可证伪性。已在多个领域铸造厂实现，包括Dick's Sporting Goods零售分析、TCC 44K经济学因果主张图谱、Indus Script科学挑战、MyFixIt程序式PSR等，MyFixIt检索实验显示结构化PSR表示显著优于token基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型的主导复用形式（更大的嵌入或扁平摘要）不适合科学、工业和运营场景。用户需要的是一个领域基础模型，能够被设计、检查、适配、刷新和信任——保留局部证据、暴露不确定性、记住来源、并说明何时不应将主张从一个语境迁移到另一个语境。Odyssey旨在解决基础模型构建中"局部性、可追溯性、可证伪性"不足的问题。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 铸造厂代数——将基础模型构建为可组合、可特化、可限制、可粘合的代数对象；(2) 五代理实现栈——Scylla(人机接口契约)、Homer(工作流编排)、Athena(层表示语义与有限真值⊥/WEAK/PLAUSIBLE/SUPPORTED/⊤)、Prometheus(拓扑斯世界模型实例化)、Toulmin(论证票)；(3) 通用铸造厂学习(UFL)——通过左Kan扩张(Lan)将局部工件滚动为候选铸造厂，通过右Kan扩张(Ran)执行限制、粘合、阻塞、论证条件；(4) TICKET(拓扑斯积分因果Kan扩张Transformer)——将外部预训练模型通过类型化映射器导入持久状态；(5) FSQL(铸造厂SQL)——类型化查询表面；(6) BRIDGE/SKFM潜在因果精炼与SkillOpt策略优化集成。

Result: 系统已全面实现并在多种铸造厂上测试：(1) TCC 44K——44K论文的295,252节点/261,714边/265,656支持行的因果主张图谱，节点边和支持查表可粘合，方向/极性/争议保持可审查；(2) Indus Script——419符号、1548视觉序列，解密争议被有意阻塞；(3) MyFixIt程序式PSR——2,224手册/53,482步骤，三种评测配置下PSR动作-观察表示的nDCG@10提升+0.25~+0.27，Recall@10从0.10提升至0.61；(4) SkillOpt优化——三个种子运行将硬评分从0.0提升到0.83~1.0；(5) 接地Toulmin——三个不同规模本地LLM(3B/80B/235B)在6个接地主张上均收敛到同一恐龙灭绝审查案例，表明阻塞源于层接地包而非特定模型；(6) Prometheus双向传输——129个Prometheus家族案例通过TICKET控制台检查(6个已接纳、6个隔离、117个遗留候选)。

Conclusion: Odyssey将基础模型构建重新定义为可检查铸造厂的设计，通过层状族保留局部性、支持度、漂移、矛盾、来源、更新义务和认知限制。BRIDGE/SKFM和IDC集成锐化了因果部分，SkillOpt实验表明系统能在保持局部授权、限定、反驳、残差和阻塞的同时学习更好的接纳策略。局限在于当前实现仍处于设计阶段，多个Scylla/Athena步骤为模板驱动，视觉和多模态接地不完整。论文将在ICML 2026以2.5小时教程形式呈现。

Abstract: We introduce a categorical framework called ODYSSEY for constructing verifiable, local truth-preserving foundation models as compositions of foundries: building-block architectural components that specify a cover of local contexts, local representation families, restriction maps, gluing rules, obstruction policies, update obligations, and human-facing views. A foundry is an organized sheaf of knowledge that carries within it an argumentation component. Concrete foundries are built from generic foundries such as evidence/argument, operational decision, institutional/financial, market meaning, scientific challenge, research-program, assistant-build, and evaluation-harness foundries. Universal Foundry Learning (UFL) formalizes foundry construction as a composition of left and right Kan extensions, with left Kan extension rolling local artifacts into candidate foundries and right Kan extension enforcing the restriction, gluing, obstruction, and argumentation conditions required for promotion. Foundry SQL (FSQL) is a small typed query surface for slicing maintained foundry artifacts that uses TICKET (Topos Integration using Causal Kan Extension Transformers) certification for admitting external or pre-built models into durable ODYSSEY state. ODYSSEY is fully implemented and tested across a wide spectrum of concrete foundries, showing that the same categorical machinery supports domain construction, artifact replay, sheaf diagnostics, grounded Toulmin/local-LLM scrutiny, residual-obstruction ledgers, and optimized TICKET-compatible causal-claim extraction across heterogeneous sources. This paper is to be presented as a 2.5 hour tutorial at ICML 2026. The tutorial home page is at this https URL .

</details>


### [65] [DysLexLens: A Low-Resource LLM Framework for Analysing Dyslexic Learners Insights from Online Forums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27619)
*Dana Rezazadegan,Atie Kia,Phongpadid Nandavong,Dominique Carlon,Jeremy Nguyen,Abhik Banerjee,James Marshall,Anthony McCosker,Yong-Bin Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Dyslexic learners increasingly use artificial intelligence (AI) tools to support reading, writing, organisation, and study-related tasks. However, their lived experiences with these tools remain largely underexamined. This paper proposes DysLexLens, a low-resource LLM framework, designed to analyse dyslexic learners experience with AI through online forum discussions. DysLexLens is designed as an end-to-end, evidence-traceable architecture which transforms noisy social media posts into a dictionary-driven corpora, provides knowledge-graph (KG)-based question reasoning, generates verifiable query responses, and enables response evaluation through quantitative and human-grounded assessment. DysLexLens has four key features. First, it employs a dictionary-driven filtering method to construct a more focused Reddit corpus on dyslexia and AI, filtering out noisy and weakly related posts to improve the relevance of data collected from low-resource forum contexts. Second, it integrates LLM-assisted semantic analysis with KG-based query reasoning to uncover meaningful patterns. Third, it has quantitative evaluation metrics (RAGAS and Query Robustness) to measure LLM-generated response performance. Fourth, it provides structured qualitative validation guidelines for assessing response quality, with a specific focus on hallucination and evidence alignment. We demonstrate the effectiveness of DysLexLens using dyslexia-related Reddit forum data and 30 questions. The results show its potential generalisability to other low-resource forum data contexts. DysLexLens, sample data, questions and evaluation results are available at Github to support reproducibility.

However, their lived experiences with these tools remain largely underexamined. This paper proposes DysLexLens, a low-resource LLM framework, designed to analyse dyslexic learners experience with AI through online forum discussions. ### [66] [MER-R1: Multimodal Emotion Reasoning via Slow-Fast Thinking Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27652)
*Zhiyuan Han,Beier Zhu,Wenwen Tong,Chengwei Qin,Xinyi Wang,Jiayu Zhang,Jiangnan Chen,Hewei Guo,Dongchuan Ran,Lewei Lu,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多模态情感识别（MER）中的"思维悖论"：在基于推理的多模态大语言模型中，启用显式推理（慢思考）反而可能不如直接输出答案（快思考）的识别准确率高，尽管推理提升了可解释性。为此提出了 MER-R1 强化学习框架，通过双目标解耦和快慢思考置信度校准实现快慢协同，在 MER-UniBench 和 MME-Emotion 上达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者在推理式多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中发现一个反直觉现象：尽管慢思考（先推理再回答）能产生良好的推理链，但在开放词汇多模态情感识别（OV-MER）任务中，其表现反而不如直接给出答案的快思考。进一步分析发现，快思考在预测层面具有更广的覆盖率和更高的召回率，在置信度上对正确类别更有信心；而慢思考则倾向于保守过滤错误类别以提升精度。当前评估主要依赖召回率指标或 F1 指标，未能充分体现慢思考精度优势，因此出现了"思维悖论"。核心动机是：能否将快思考的召回能力与慢思考的精度选择性有机结合，让推理真正有益于情感识别？

Method: MER-R1 是一个基于 GRPO 的强化学习框架，包含两大核心组件：(1) 双目标解耦（Dual-objective Disentanglement）—— 将 F1 奖励分解为独立的召回奖励和精度奖励，并在奖励和优势空间分别进行组归一化，避免在聚合时因方差差异导致某一目标被压制。理论证明（F1 优势相关性偏向归一化方差较大的目标，τ>1 时偏向召回，τ≤1 时偏向精度），而双目标优势则保证两者与优势的相关系数严格相等。(2) 快慢置信度校准（Slow-Fast Confidence Calibration）—— 对每个生成的情绪词，比较快慢思考在情感轮 level-1 类别上的置信度差异 Δ_i(e)；对正确类别鼓励 Δ_i(e)>0（保留快思考自信），对错误类别鼓励 Δ_i(e)<0（保留慢思考抑制），通过双向校准实现对正确情感的强化和错误情感的抑制。最终优势由 A_dual + λ_cali·A_cali + λ_fmt·A_fmt 三部分组合。基座模型采用 Qwen2.5-Omni，分两阶段训练（SFT 2 epoch + GRPO RL 1 epoch，4 采样/题）。

Result: 在 MER-UniBench（9 个数据集）上达到 83.50 的最先进平均分，较最强复现基线提升 5.63 分，其中 OV-MERD+ 从 66.86 提升至 70.68；在 MME-Emotion 上以 51.5 的 CoT 均分刷新最优成绩，识别分从 27.9 跃升至 38.4。关键地，MER-R1 成功逆转了思维悖论：在统一 F1 评估下，其慢思考最终答案在所有 5 个识别数据集上均优于快思考（R-Mean 60.86→61.80），而基线和 VideoAuto-R1 仍存在慢思考弱于快思考的现象。消融实验验证了奖励解耦（RD）、优势解耦（AD）、置信度校准（SFCC）三部分均贡献显著；类级别置信度粒度优于词级，优势空间校准优于奖励空间校准。定性分析展示三类协同行为：快思考直觉保留、虚假情绪抑制、遗漏情绪恢复。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地揭示并解决了多模态情感识别中的思维悖论：通过形式化慢快思考的互补性（快思考贡献召回直觉与正确类别自信，慢思考贡献精度选择性与错误抑制），提出了双目标解耦和快慢置信度校准两个机制，让慢思考真正服务于识别任务而非仅仅提供可解释性。MER-R1 在两个权威基准上刷新 SOTA，验证了所提两项期待（Desiderata）可同时满足，为推理式 MLLM 在开放词汇任务中的优化提供了新范式。局限性：主要在情感基准上验证、广义到其他多模态推理任务有待探索；依赖情感轮类别映射限制了细粒度覆盖；训练时需要额外的快思考前向传播增加了计算成本。

Abstract: We find that explicit reasoning does not necessarily translate into better multimodal emotion recognition (MER) accuracy, even though it makes predictions more interpretable. Specifically, for reasoning-based MLLMs, fast thinking by triggering direct answers often outperforms slow thinking after deliberative reasoning. Our empirical analyses show that fast thinking improves recall with broader and more confident predictions, whereas slow thinking favors precision through conservative filtering of incorrect categories. Building on these insights, we propose MER-R1, a reinforcement learning framework that turns slow-fast complementarity into explicit optimization. Dual-objective disentanglement separates recall and precision into two optimization signals, allowing them to be jointly optimized rather than traded off against each other. Slow-fast confidence calibration further aligns the final slow-thinking answer with fast-thinking intuition, strengthening correct emotions while suppressing incorrect ones. In this way, MER-R1 unifies the recall-oriented intuition of fast thinking with the precision-oriented selectivity of slow thinking. We further provide theoretical justification for this synergy, showing that it mitigates variance-induced interference during optimization. Extensive experiments on MER-UniBench and MME-Emotion show that MER-R1 achieves state-of-the-art performance and makes reasoning genuinely benefit emotion recognition.

</details>


### [67] [ToE: A Hierarchical and Explainable Claim Verification Framework with Dynamic Multi-source Evidence Retrieval and Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27736)
*Zhaoqi Wang,Zijian Zhang,Kun Zheng,Zhen Li,Xin Li,Chunlei Li,Jiamou Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ToE（Tree of Evidence）框架，将事实核查建模为动态扩展的论证树，集成强化学习驱动的多源检索Agent、证据评估Agent和树聚合算法，迭代分解、检索并验证声明，生成可解释的证据链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: （1）AI生成虚假信息与GEO（Generative Engine Optimization）投毒攻击使恶意内容可被检索系统系统性提升排名，污染LLM推理上下文；（2）现有LLM事实核查方法依赖静态知识、缺乏实时外部信息，对风格流畅但事实错误的AI生成虚假内容辨别力不足；（3）深度学习方法跨域泛化能力差，且缺乏可解释性。

Method: ToE框架包含三大核心组件：（1）**RL驱动的多源检索Agent**：状态为29维向量（包含声明类别、地理范围、检索进度等），动作空间含8个离散动作（通用网页、反驳证据、arXiv、Wikipedia、社交媒体、PolitiFact/Snopes、Reddit、停止），使用GRPO算法训练，奖励函数融合可靠性增益、真实性变化、新证据数和高置信度奖励；（2）**证据评估Agent**：基于立场感知的多路径注意力机制，将证据按支持/中立/反驳分组处理，经质量门控、掩码多头自注意力和融合网络，输出真实性分数和可靠性分数；（3）**论证树聚合算法**：自底向上聚合子节点分数，若可靠性不足则按地理、实体、事件、因果等多维度分解为2-4个子声明，置信度高时剪枝；理论方面将检索过程建模为POMDP，在可靠性函数子模性假设下证明贪婪策略性能不低于最优策略的(1-1/e)，并推导策略级价值差距上界为4Tε。

Result: 在LIAR、PolitiFact、Check-COVID及自建AdvFact对抗数据集上，ToE在DeepSeek-V3.2和GPT-OSS-20B两种骨干LLM上均取得4-24个百分点的准确率提升，在AdvFact上提升尤为显著（DeepSeek:0.64 vs STEEL 0.16；GPT-OSS:0.54 vs AdSent 0.52）；检索工具消融实验表明，移除事实核查来源导致整体准确率从80.0%骤降至56.6%，为影响最大的组件；动作分布热力图显示检索Agent具备领域自适应行为（科学声明偏好arXiv占26.4%，政治/健康声明偏好事实核查占35.5%/34.5%，名人声明偏好社交媒体占24.5%）。

Conclusion: ToE通过将证据组织为分层论证树并结合训练后的智能体引导检索和证据评分，成功将复杂声明分解为可验证的子论证并聚合证据，实验证明该方法在多个数据集和声明域上具有良好泛化能力，为抵御GEO投毒的证据驱动声明验证提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The rapid spread of fake news poses increasing threats to information ecosystems, especially as AI-generated misinformation under Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) poisoning allows adversarially crafted content to be systematically surfaced by retrieval systems, contaminating LLM reasoning. In this paper, we propose Tree of Evidence (ToE), a hierarchical evidence reasoning framework for automated fact-checking that models each claim as a dynamically expanding argument tree. ToE integrates a reinforcement learning-driven multi-source retrieval agent, an evidence evaluation agent, and an argument tree aggregation algorithm to iteratively decompose, retrieve, and verify claims through an explainable evidence chain. We further provide a theoretical analysis of the retrieval process, deriving a formal error bound that guarantees the learned policy converges to a neighborhood of the information-theoretically optimal policy. Experiments across multiple datasets and backbone LLMs demonstrate that ToE achieves improvements ranging from 4 to 24 percentage points over competitive baselines, with particularly pronounced gains on adversarially poisoned inputs.

</details>


### [68] [Towards Reliable and Robust LLM Planning: Symbolic Feedback-Driven Iterative Self-Refinement Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27757)
*Jiajing Zhang,Jiamei Jiang,Chenyang Zhang,Feifei Mo,Linjing Li,Daniel Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种**符号反馈驱动的迭代自优化框架**，通过将神经-符号方法结合，将PDDL符号映射为自然语言提示，并利用符号验证器（包含语法检查、语义一致性检查和基于规划识别的目标可达性推理）为LLM提供精确的错误反馈，引导其迭代修正长horizon规划中的错误，从而提升LLM规划的可行性、最优性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽然具备强大的语言理解能力，但在长horizon规划任务中常常产生不可行或不正确的方案，源于幻觉、对长时序依赖建模不足及组合复杂性等问题；而经典符号规划器虽然严谨但缺乏灵活性，难以处理自然语言输入。两者之间的互补性促使研究者构建神经-符号融合框架，利用符号方法的精确验证信号指导LLM规划，同时保留LLM的语言灵活性，以构建更可信、更安全的AI系统。

Method: 1. **自然语言提示机制**：将PDDL中的符号（动作、前条件、效果、谓词等）映射为自然语言描述，保留逻辑约束的同时降低LLM理解符号语言的门槛；2. **反馈驱动的迭代自优化框架**：构建"提示→生成→验证→反馈→修正"的闭环，反馈增强提示由T_input⊕T_feedback⊕T_history三部分组成，最大迭代5轮；3. **基于规划识别的符号验证器**：分层多维度检查——语法正确性（T_syn）、语义一致性（使用VAL工具验证，T_sem）、目标可达性（基于landmark方法的规划识别器推断最可能目标G_inf并与真实目标G_tru比对，T_recg），输出三类反馈指导LLM针对性修正。

Result: 在PlanBench基准（1,200个任务）的Blocksworld和Mystery两个领域上测试GPT-4o、Claude-3-5和DeepSeek-R1三种LLM：(1) **整体性能提升显著**——GPT-4o在Blocksworld覆盖率从35.5%升至69.5%（↑34.0%），Claude-3-5从54.8%升至83.5%（↑28.7%），DeepSeek-R1从99.1%达到100%；在无显式语义的Mystery域中GPT-4o和Claude-3-5从0%分别提升至3.8%和4.0%，DeepSeek-R1从43.3%提升至65.5%（↑22.2%）；(2) **消融实验**：NL提示使可行性最多提升15.2%、最优性最多提升6.5%；在此基础上加入增强反馈，可行性再最多提升14.5%、最优性再最多提升9.0%；(3) **规划长度分析**：在长度2-14范围内反馈增强模型持续优于基线，且随难度增加差距扩大；当token限制从1024扩展到2048时，反馈增强模型获得显著增益而基线几乎无改善；(4) **反馈策略灵活性**：找到可行解即停止获得71.3%可行/50.7%最优覆盖；找到最优解才停止则获得63.2%可行/54.7%最优覆盖，可根据需求灵活权衡。

Conclusion: 该框架通过神经-符号融合有效提升了LLM在长horizon规划任务中的可行性、最优性和鲁棒性，为构建更可靠、可信的AI系统提供了可行路径；符号验证器提供的分层精确反馈是性能提升的关键，自然语言提示机制有效缓解了LLM对符号语言的解析障碍；未来工作将把该框架扩展到更广泛的开放和动态环境中进行评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have attracted widespread attention from academia and industry, yet their deployment raises critical security concerns regarding robustness and reliability. Planning, a core component of intelligent behavior, remains challenging for LLMs, which often produce infeasible or incorrect solutions in long-horizon decision-making tasks due to inherent complexity. In this paper, we propose a symbolic feedback-driven iterative self-refinement framework to enhance the robustness and reliability of LLMs in long-horizon planning. Specifically, a natural language prompting mechanism is introduced to map logical symbols into natural language descriptions, enabling LLMs to better capture task constraints and semantics. We further design a symbolic verifier that identifies errors and converts them into corrective instructions interpretable by the LLM, thereby guiding self-refinement. In addition, we leverage a plan recognizer to infer goal reachability, facilitating more effective guidance toward desired goals. Empirical results demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves both feasibility and correctness in long-horizon planning tasks. This highlights its effectiveness in enhancing the reliability of LLM-based planning and potential to enable more trustworthy AI systems.

</details>


### [69] [Understanding Rollout Error in Graph World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27780)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: World models are often used for planning by rolling learned dynamics forward. Many planning environments, however, are not vectors or images; they are graphs of agents, tools, skills, routes, and dependencies. In these settings, a local prediction error may stay local or spread through the graph, and the failure mode changes again when edges are predicted rather than fixed. This paper studies long-horizon rollout error in Graph World Models (GWMs). We formulate a unified fixed-edge and dynamic-edge GWM framework with action nodes for node-, edge-, and graph-level decisions. We develop graph-valued rollout bounds that separate topology-induced amplification from model-induced amplification, and we introduce a joint node-edge operator for dynamic-edge rollouts. Guided by the analysis, we propose Error-Aware GWM, which combines spectral regularization, rollout consistency, and critical-node weighting. Across synthetic topologies and heterogeneous agent-graph testbeds, rollout error and planning regret grow with horizon, dynamic-edge training is needed when structure evolves, and Error-Aware GWM prevents long-horizon divergence while preserving prediction accuracy. Real-world graph benchmarks clarify the scope of GWMs: they are most useful for dynamic graph rollout and agent planning, while specialized graph models remain strong on static or sparse prediction tasks.

Abstract: World models are often used for planning by rolling learned dynamics forward. Many planning environments, however, are not vectors or images; they are graphs of agents, tools, skills, routes, and dependencies. In these settings, a local prediction error may stay local or spread through the graph, and the failure mode changes again when edges are predicted rather than fixed.
